# App Shell and Lab Canvas Implementation Plan (sub-project 3)

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** The first usable product. A Next.js app (`apps/web`) where a learner builds a system on a canvas, runs it on our wasm engine in a Web Worker, and watches it play back, plus the app shell (navigation, command palette, language, theme, help) that sub-projects 4–6 plug into.

**Architecture:** Next.js 15 App Router under `/[locale]/…` (`vi`, `en`) with `next-intl`. The Lab is one client component tree. Its state lives in three Zustand stores: `design` (graph, layout, groups, workload, faults, world parameters, with undo/redo), `run` (the last 10 results), and `playback` (the clock). A dedicated Web Worker owns the wasm instance from `@sd/sim/web` and answers typed messages (`validate`, `run`, `trace`, `listComponents`, `defaultCalibration`, `capabilities`, …). React Flow 12 renders the canvas. A single `<canvas>` layer above it draws the request dots. Designs, presets and the autosaved draft live in IndexedDB. Content (curriculum, challenges) is read from `content/` by server components at build or request time and handed to the client as plain props.

**Tech Stack:** Docker dev container (`./dev <cmd>`, Rust 1.85.1, Node 24, pnpm 10, Playwright Chromium baked into the image); Next.js 15.5.27, React 19.3.0, TypeScript 5.9.3, `@xyflow/react` 12.12.0, `elkjs` 0.12.0, Zustand 5.0.15, Tailwind CSS 4.3.3, Radix UI primitives, `lucide-react` 1.52.0, `next-intl` 4.14.9, `uplot` 1.6.32, `idb` 8.0.3, `cmdk` 1.1.1, `fuse.js` 7.5.0, `zod` 4.6.5; Vitest 5.0.3 + Testing Library + jsdom; Playwright 1.63.0 + `@axe-core/playwright` 4.13.0.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-20-app-lab-design.md`

**Previous plans (style, engine and content APIs):** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-18-sim-engine-1a.md`, `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-20-content-format.md`

**Feature checklist:** `docs/feature-parity.md` (rows marked 3 or A; mapping at the end of this plan)

## Rulings on spec ambiguities

The spec leaves these points open. Each ruling below is what this plan implements. The owner can overturn any of them before Phase 1 starts.

| # | Topic | Ruling |
|---|---|---|
| R1 | Engine change | The only engine change is a `capabilities()` export in `crates/sim-wasm` (Task 1). It serializes `sim_core::capabilities()` through a local `CapabilitiesJs` struct, so `sim-core` gains no `Serialize` derive and does not change at all. `@sd/sim` gains `Sim.capabilities()` and a hand-written `Capabilities` TS type. |
| R2 | How the wasm is loaded and served | The worker imports `loadSim` from `@sd/sim/web` and calls it **without** a URL. The wasm-bindgen `web` glue then runs `new URL('sim_wasm_bg.wasm', import.meta.url)`. Webpack 5 (which Next uses) treats that expression as an asset reference: it copies the file to `.next/static/media/sim_wasm_bg.<hash>.wasm` and rewrites the URL to `/_next/static/media/sim_wasm_bg.<hash>.wasm`. Next serves that path with `Content-Type: application/wasm` and an immutable cache header, so `WebAssembly.instantiateStreaming` works and a new engine build gets a new URL. The worker is created with `new Worker(new URL("./sim.worker.ts", import.meta.url), { type: "module" })`, which webpack bundles as its own chunk. `next.config.ts` sets `transpilePackages: ["@sd/sim", "@sd/content"]` because both export TypeScript source. **Turbopack is not used** (`next dev` and `next build` without `--turbopack`), because this wiring is only verified for webpack. A Playwright smoke test (Task 7) proves all of it in Chromium: the worker reports the engine version, the `.wasm` response is 200 with `application/wasm`, and there are no console errors. **Fallback, only if that smoke test shows the asset is not emitted:** Task 7 Step 9 copies the file to `public/engine/sim_wasm_bg.<sha256-12>.wasm` in `ensure-wasm.mjs`, writes the URL to a generated (gitignored) `src/engine/wasm-url.ts`, and the worker passes it to `loadSim(url)`. |
| R3 | Building the wasm before the app | `packages/sim/wasm/` stays gitignored. `apps/web/scripts/ensure-wasm.mjs` runs before `dev`, `build` and `test`. It runs `pnpm --filter @sd/sim build:wasm` only when `packages/sim/wasm/web/sim_wasm_bg.wasm` or `packages/sim/wasm/node/sim_wasm_bg.wasm` is missing, or is older than the newest file under `crates/sim-core/src`, `crates/sim-wasm/src`, `crates/sim-*/Cargo.toml` and `Cargo.lock`. |
| R4 | Playwright in Docker | **Browsers are installed in the dev image**, not run from the official Playwright image. The dev image already has Rust, wasm-pack, Node and the workspace volumes. The e2e run needs the wasm build, `next build` and `next start` in the same container as the browser. A second image (Ubuntu-based) would need the build output and `node_modules` volumes shared across two images with different base OSes. Only Chromium is installed (`npx playwright@1.63.0 install --with-deps chromium`, browsers in `/ms-playwright`). The image tag goes from `system-design-dev:1` to `:2`, so `./dev` rebuilds stale local images. A unit test fails when the Dockerfile's Playwright version differs from `@playwright/test` in `apps/web/package.json`. Fonts come from npm packages (`@fontsource/*`), so screenshots do not depend on system fonts. |
| R5 | `web` compose service | `web` `extends` `dev`, runs `pnpm --filter @sd/web dev` (`next dev -H 0.0.0.0 -p 3000`), and publishes `3000:3000`. New named volumes: `node-modules-web` (`/work/apps/web/node_modules`) and `web-next` (`/work/apps/web/.next`; keeps the build cache off the macOS bind mount). `NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1` is set in the image, because the app must not call external services. |
| R6 | Routing and locale | `next-intl` routing: locales `["vi", "en"]`, default `vi`, `localePrefix: "always"`, locale cookie `NEXT_LOCALE` kept for a year. The middleware picks the browser language on a first visit (`Accept-Language`) and the cookie afterwards. The language switch sets the cookie and replaces the route with the same path in the other locale. `/` and `/{locale}` redirect to `/{locale}/lab`. |
| R7 | Message keys | Catalogs are nested JSON. An engine code `a.b` is the nested key `a → b`. Top-level namespaces: `common`, `meta`, `nav`, `language`, `theme`, `help`, `shell`, `lab`, `palette`, `inspector`, `workload`, `faults`, `world`, `run`, `playback`, `trace`, `panel`, `designs`, `importIssue`, `tour`, `shortcuts`, `learn`, `challenges`, `leaderboard`, `component` (by kind, from `labelKey`), `componentDescription`, `category`, `role`, `edgeKind`, `configField`, `configOption`, `override`, `calibration`, `profile`, `arrivals`, `fault` (by kind), `metric`, `timelineField`, `traceOutcome`, `traceAction`, `engine.event`, `engine.validation`, `unit`. |
| R8 | Every engine code is translated | `src/i18n/engine-codes.ts` lists every event and validation code. A unit test reads `crates/sim-core/src/**/*.rs` (non-test code), extracts event codes (`event(…, "x.y", …)` and `code: "x.y"`) and the validation codes (the `pub mod codes` constants), and fails when the list and the engine differ. A second test fails when either catalog misses a key for any of: the event and validation codes; all 14 fault kinds; every `labelKey`, description, category, config field and choice option from `listComponents()`; every `capabilities().nodeOverrideKeys` and `workloadParams` entry; every `defaultCalibration()` field; every `Metrics` key; every profile, arrival mode, edge kind, timeline field, trace outcome and trace action. The two catalogs must have the same key set and the same ICU argument names per key. A Vietnamese value longer than 12 characters that equals the English value fails, unless its key is listed in `src/i18n/same-in-both.ts`. This catches English left inside Vietnamese text. |
| R9 | Hard-coded strings | ESLint `i18next/no-literal-string` (eslint-plugin-i18next 6.1.5), `mode: "jsx-only"`. It flags JSX text and the user-facing attributes `aria-label`, `aria-description`, `aria-roledescription`, `aria-valuetext`, `aria-placeholder`, `title`, `placeholder`, `alt`, `label` in `app/**/*.tsx` and `src/**/*.tsx`. Tests are excluded. A unit test lints a fixture through the ESLint API and proves the rule fires. Strings outside JSX (toast text, `document.title`) must go through `t()`. That is a review item, because the rule cannot see them. |
| R10 | Per-node monthly cost | The engine has no per-node cost API, and adding one would change `sim-core`. The worker computes it with the existing `simulate`: for each non-client node, a 100 ms run of `client → node` (edge kind = the node's first accepted kind, `baseRps: 0`) with the design's calibration. Its `costPerMonth` is the node's cost. A unit test checks that the per-node costs sum to the full design's `costPerMonth`. A node whose config the engine rejects shows "—". |
| R11 | Live validation | The worker's `validate` message runs `validateGraph(graph, limits)` (limits come from a challenge, else `null`) and computes the node costs. It is debounced 150 ms after each change. Workload and world-parameter inputs enforce their own bounds. Anything the engine still rejects at run time (`workload.invalid`, `calibration.invalid`) arrives as `SimValidationError` and is shown in the same problems list. Run is disabled while a blocking issue exists. |
| R12 | Design model | A design stores nodes with **absolute** positions, edges, groups, workload, faults, calibration overrides, duration, seed, locked node ids and an optional challenge reference (`designFormatVersion: 1`). New node ids are `<kind>-<n>` (`web-server-1`), edge ids `e<n>`, group ids `g<n>`; `n` is the lowest unused number. A new node's `label` is `""`, and the UI shows the translated component name plus `n`, so a label never freezes one language. **Locked** (from a challenge): cannot be deleted, renamed or reconfigured, and replicas cannot change. It can be moved, and its edges are free. This matches content ruling R15. |
| R13 | Connecting | A drag or the keyboard picker creates an edge whose kind is inferred: `db` when the target's role is `database`, otherwise `http` (spec §5.1). A target that accepts no edge kind (a client) refuses the connection and shows the translated `edge.not-accepted` message. Self-loops and duplicate `from→to` pairs are refused silently. A kind the target does not accept can still be chosen in the inspector, and validation then flags it. |
| R14 | Edges and layout | Orthogonal edges are React Flow `smoothstep` edges (`borderRadius: 8`). Auto-layout uses elkjs `layered`, direction `RIGHT`, `elk.edgeRouting: ORTHOGONAL`. Only node positions are taken from ELK; React Flow draws the edges. Nodes are 184 × 76 px for layout. Snap grid is 16 px. |
| R15 | Groups | A group is a React Flow node of type `group` (a labelled frame). Its members get `parentId` and a position relative to the group, converted from and to the absolute positions in the design. Dragging the frame moves the members. Grouping the selection makes a frame 32 px around the selected nodes' bounding box. Ungroup keeps the members' absolute positions. Groups never reach `SimInput`. |
| R16 | Undo/redo | The history keeps up to 200 design snapshots (spec: at least 100). A drag is one step, committed on drag stop. Selection, viewport and run results are not in the history. Undo/redo of a locked node is impossible because locked nodes cannot change. |
| R17 | Config fields | The engine's `FieldKind` is `number` or `choice` today. `number` renders a stepper (step 1 when `integer`, else 0.5) clamped to `min..max`. `choice` renders a select. A field whose `default` is a boolean renders a switch (none exist yet; covered by a unit test with a synthetic field). Labels come from `configField.<key>`, options from `configOption.<key>.<option>`. |
| R18 | Running | ⌘↵ / Ctrl+↵ or the Run button. The worker keeps the `SimRun` handles of the last 10 runs (for `trace(i)` and JSON export) and frees older ones. The main thread keeps the same 10 results as plain data (metrics, events, timeline header + transferred `Float32Array`, trace summaries, input, elapsed ms). A new run selects itself and resets playback to 0 s, paused. |
| R19 | Playback | 1× plays one simulated second per real second. Speeds are 0.25, 0.5, 1, 2, 4. The frame shown is `floor(t / frameMs)`. Node state follows spec §5.4: `down` when `up = 0` (the engine reports 1 while any instance is up), `overloaded` when `rejected > 0` or `busy ≥ 0.95`, `busy` when `busy ≥ 0.7`. The engine timeline has no per-edge throughput, so dot density is a **visual approximation**: an edge's rate at frame *k* is its target's `inFlight` divided by the target's in-degree, scaled so the busiest edge in the run gets 12 dots per second of simulated time. Every edge with traffic gets at least 1. Playback never starts on its own (R18). With `prefers-reduced-motion`, dots move without trails and the overloaded pulse is off. |
| R20 | Faults | All 14 kinds are listed. Kinds missing from `capabilities().faults` are disabled with a "coming soon" tooltip. Targets are filtered by role: `instance-down`, `instance-slow` → service; `lb-node-down` → balancer; `db-*` → database; `traffic-surge-3x` → no target; the other kinds need components that do not exist yet. The first target option is "any matching (the engine picks)" (`target: null`). "Random fault" uses `mulberry32(design.seed + 7919 × faults.length)` to pick a supported kind, a matching target and a time between 20% and 70% of the duration, rounded to 100 ms. The same design always draws the same sequence. |
| R21 | Traffic presets | Four built-in presets (our own numbers): quiet (200 rps, 10% writes), lunch peak (3,000 rps, 20%), flash sale (8,000 rps, 30%, surge ×3 at 4 s for 3 s), write storm (2,000 rps, 70%). Users save the current workload as a named preset in IndexedDB. Applying a preset replaces the workload fields and keeps duration, seed and params. |
| R22 | Storage | IndexedDB database `sd-lab`, version 1, stores `designs` (key `id`, index `updatedAt`), `presets` (key `id`) and `kv` (`draft`, `settings`). No count limit. The draft autosaves 1 s after the last change. On open, the Lab restores the draft unless a challenge or template was requested in the URL. |
| R23 | Design file | Export writes `<name>.sdlab.json`: the design with `designFormatVersion: 1`, plus `engineVersion` (informational only). Import validates with a zod schema. Each problem is shown as `<path>: <translated reason>`, with reasons from `importIssue.<zod issue code>`. An unknown component kind is accepted on import and then flagged by validation as `node.unknown-kind`. |
| R24 | Content in the app | Server components read `content/curriculum.json` and `content/challenges/<id>/challenge.json` from the repo (`path.resolve(process.cwd(), "../../content")`). Challenge ids must match `^c-[a-z0-9-]+$` (no path traversal). `start` is normalized with sim-core's serde defaults (label `""`, replicas 1, config `{}`, profile `mixed`, writeRatio 0.2, clients 1000, clientTimeoutMs 1000, duration 10 000, seed **1** per content ruling R2). The chosen mode (`?mode=`, default the first) applies `workloadOverride` (shallow merge; `null` removes a key) and `faults`. `limits` go to `validateGraph`, and the palette greys out kinds outside `allowedKinds`. Node positions come from auto-layout. |
| R25 | Search | The command palette (cmdk, `shouldFilter={false}`) ranks with fuse.js over text folded to lowercase ASCII (NFD with combining marks removed, `đ → d`). Every item indexes both its Vietnamese and English text, so either language finds it. Items: Lab commands, every lesson and challenge in the curriculum (titles plus concept tags), and every component kind with its description. Searching lesson text and engine docs waits for sub-project 4 (no lesson pages yet). |
| R26 | Theme | `data-theme="light|dark"` on `<html>`. The preference (`light`, `dark`, `system`; default `system`) lives in `localStorage["sd-theme"]`. An inline script in `<head>` applies it before first paint. With `system`, a `matchMedia` listener follows OS changes live. Tailwind's `dark:` variant is bound to `[data-theme=dark]`. |
| R27 | Onboarding tour | Our own seven-step tour built on Radix Popover, anchored to `[data-tour="…"]` elements. It starts on the first Lab visit (`localStorage["sd-tour-lab"]` unset) and can be replayed from the help menu. Escape or "Skip" ends it and marks it seen. |
| R28 | CI | New `.github/workflows/web.yml`. Path filters are spec §9 (`apps/web/**`, `packages/**`, `crates/sim-wasm/**`, `crates/sim-core/**`, `content/**`, the workflow file) **plus** the build files (`Dockerfile`, `compose.yaml`, `compose.ci.yaml`, `package.json`, `pnpm-lock.yaml`, `pnpm-workspace.yaml`), because a dependency or image change can break the app. The dev image is built with `docker/build-push-action` and a GitHub Actions layer cache; the Playwright browsers live in a cached layer (R4). `compose.ci.yaml` bind-mounts host directories over the pnpm store, the cargo registry and target, and `apps/web/.next`, and `actions/cache` saves them. `engine.yml` switches to the same cached image build, because the image now also installs Chromium. |
| R29 | Test layout | Unit and component tests sit next to their code as `*.test.ts(x)`. They use the `node` environment by default, and component tests opt into jsdom with `// @vitest-environment jsdom`. Engine-backed unit tests use `@sd/sim/node` (same wasm, Node target). E2E tests live in `apps/web/e2e/`. Screenshot baselines live in `apps/web/e2e/__screenshots__/`, are produced only in the container (Linux, Chromium) and are committed through a `.gitignore` exception for `*.png`. Vitest is 5.0.3 in `apps/web`. `packages/sim` keeps its existing 3.2.7. |
| R30 | Performance targets (spec §7) | Measured by `e2e/perf.spec.ts` against `next start` in the container. Lab first load: navigation start → engine status `ready`, under 2 500 ms. Run: the worker round trip for the committed 30-node fixture, under 50 ms (median of 5). Playback: the median `requestAnimationFrame` interval over 180 frames with 30 nodes playing at 1×, at most 17.5 ms (≥ 57 fps). The numbers and the machine go into `docs/perf/lab.md`. A miss is investigated and reported, never relaxed. |
| R31 | Desktop only | Below 1 280 px wide, a dismissible "best on a desktop" banner shows, and the page still renders. Nothing is hidden. |
| R32 | Commit messages | Each task gives its commit message. The plan's messages carry no trailer lines. Trailers follow the executing agent's standing instructions. |

## Global Constraints

- **Every build/test command runs inside the dev container via `./dev <command>`** (for example `./dev pnpm --filter @sd/web test`). Nothing is installed on the host. `docker compose up web` is the one long-running command developers start directly.
- `sim-core` does not change. `sim-wasm` gains only `capabilities()` (R1).
- The app never calls the reference site or any external service at runtime: no remote fonts, no analytics, no telemetry (`NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1`), no CDN scripts. `packages/sim/test/no-external-calls.test.ts` keeps scanning `apps/`.
- All user-facing text comes from `apps/web/messages/{vi,en}.json` through `next-intl`. The engine emits codes; the UI translates them. Both catalogs are written separately in natural language; neither is a line-by-line translation of the other. No text, image, icon or styling is copied from the reference product or from books.
- Every interactive control is reachable by keyboard and has an accessible name. Focus is always visible (2 px `--sd-focus` ring, 2 px offset).
- Exact dependency versions only (table above). Do not upgrade during the plan. A broken pin is reported, not silently bumped.
- TypeScript `strict` and `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`. No `any` in `src/` except at the worker message boundary, where values are typed right after receipt.
- Runtime code in `apps/web/src` must not import `@sd/sim/node` (tests only). It imports `@sd/sim` for types and `@sd/sim/web` only inside the worker.
- Before each commit: `./dev pnpm --filter @sd/web lint && ./dev pnpm --filter @sd/web typecheck && ./dev pnpm --filter @sd/web test`.
- A phase ends with the full suite green: `./dev pnpm test` and `./dev pnpm test:web`.
- Commit messages are given in each task (R32).

## Review Focus

1. **The wasm in the browser.** The worker loads the wasm from the hashed `/_next/static/media/*.wasm` URL with `application/wasm`, in `next dev` and `next start`. A failed load or a worker crash shows a retry banner, and retry works (Task 7).
2. **No engine prose, no missing key.** Every engine code has a key in both languages; no English inside Vietnamese text; the lint rule fires (Tasks 4, 8).
3. **Undo/redo and locking.** No operation changes a locked node except moving it; undo restores exactly the previous snapshot; a drag is one step (Task 10).
4. **`SimInput` building.** Groups, positions and UI-only fields never reach the engine; unchanged world parameters are not sent; challenge starts keep their seed of 1 (Tasks 18, 31).
5. **Worker message lifetimes.** Timeline buffers are transferred, not copied. Stale validate replies are dropped. Old `SimRun` handles are freed (Tasks 7, 14, 22).
6. **Playback performance.** The dots layer does not allocate per frame and does not re-render React per frame (Task 27).
7. **Import safety.** A hostile `.sdlab.json` (wrong types, huge arrays, unknown keys, prototype keys) gives a translated issue list and never crashes or pollutes state (Task 30).

## Visual design

The look is our own. The idea is a **lab bench**: warm, quiet surfaces in light mode, ink-blue surfaces in dark mode, one teal signal colour for action and focus, and colour reserved for meaning (edge kinds, node states). Nothing is decorative. Both themes are designed separately: in dark mode, borders and slightly raised surfaces do the work that shadows do in light mode, and every hue is retuned for the dark background instead of inverted.

### Colour tokens

Defined once in `apps/web/src/ui/tokens.css` as `--sd-*` custom properties: light values on `:root`, dark values on `[data-theme="dark"]`. Tailwind maps them in `@theme inline` (Task 5), so classes read `bg-surface`, `text-text-2`, `border-border`, `stroke-edge-db`. A unit test checks the contrast pairs listed under the table (all values below pass; measured while writing this plan).

| Token | Light | Dark | Use |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--sd-bg` | `#F6F5F1` | `#0E1216` | app background, docked panels |
| `--sd-canvas` | `#FAF9F6` | `#0B0F12` | canvas background |
| `--sd-grid` | `#DEDAD0` | `#1F262D` | canvas dot grid |
| `--sd-surface` | `#FFFFFF` | `#151B21` | cards, nodes, inputs, menus |
| `--sd-surface-2` | `#F1EFEA` | `#1B232A` | hover rows, panel headers |
| `--sd-surface-3` | `#E7E4DC` | `#232C34` | pressed, selected rows |
| `--sd-border` | `#DAD6CC` | `#2A343D` | dividers, card borders |
| `--sd-border-strong` | `#8A8578` | `#64717D` | input and control borders (≥ 3:1) |
| `--sd-text` | `#1B1E22` | `#E8ECEF` | primary text |
| `--sd-text-2` | `#454A52` | `#B4BDC6` | secondary text |
| `--sd-text-3` | `#5B6068` | `#8F99A3` | captions, placeholders (≥ 4.5:1 on every surface) |
| `--sd-accent` | `#0A6970` | `#3CC4CC` | primary buttons, links, active tab, selection |
| `--sd-accent-hover` | `#08575D` | `#63D3D9` | hover on accent |
| `--sd-accent-soft` | `#DCEEEF` | `#0F3639` | selected palette item, highlighted trace path halo |
| `--sd-on-accent` | `#FFFFFF` | `#061315` | text on accent |
| `--sd-focus` | `#0A6970` | `#3CC4CC` | focus ring |
| `--sd-ok` | `#1C7A4B` | `#45C285` | passing values, "delivered" |
| `--sd-busy` | `#A86400` | `#F2A93B` | node busy (amber), warnings |
| `--sd-busy-soft` | `#FBEFD9` | `#36280F` | busy node fill |
| `--sd-over` | `#C0362C` | `#FF6B5E` | overloaded (red), blocking issues, errors |
| `--sd-over-soft` | `#FBE3E0` | `#3A1815` | overloaded node fill |
| `--sd-down` | `#737782` | `#7D8691` | down (grey) |
| `--sd-down-soft` | `#ECECEF` | `#1D232A` | down node fill |
| `--sd-info` | `#2E5BC9` | `#7A9DFF` | info log level, neutral events |
| `--sd-edge-http` | `#3360D4` | `#7A9DFF` | http edges and dots |
| `--sd-edge-db` | `#8446C9` | `#B992F4` | db edges and dots |
| `--sd-edge-cache` | `#0C8763` | `#41CC9D` | cache edges and dots |
| `--sd-edge-msg` | `#BE3A82` | `#F17DBB` | messaging edges and dots |
| `--sd-cat-general` | `#5B6470` | `#9AA4AE` | icon tile, category "general" |
| `--sd-cat-compute` | `#0A6970` | `#3CC4CC` | icon tile, category "compute" |
| `--sd-cat-data` | `#7443B5` | `#B992F4` | icon tile, category "data" |
| `--sd-scrim` | `rgb(27 30 34 / 0.40)` | `rgb(0 0 0 / 0.60)` | dialog backdrop |

Contrast checked by `src/ui/tokens.test.ts`: `text`, `text-2`, `text-3` on `bg`, `canvas`, `surface`, `surface-2`, `surface-3` ≥ 4.5; `on-accent` on `accent` ≥ 4.5; `accent` on `surface` ≥ 4.5; `ok`, `busy`, `over`, `info` on `surface` ≥ 4.5; `down`, every edge colour, every category colour and `border-strong` on `surface` and `canvas` ≥ 3; `busy`/`over`/`down` on their `-soft` fills ≥ 3; `focus` on `bg` ≥ 3.

Edge kinds are never told apart by colour alone. Each also has a dash pattern: http solid, db `8 4`, cache `2 4` (dotted, round caps), msg `10 4 2 4`. The legend shows colour and dash together. Node states also carry an icon (busy: gauge, overloaded: triangle-alert, down: power-off) and a text label in the tooltip.

### Type

- UI font: **Be Vietnam Pro** (400, 500, 600, 700), bundled from `@fontsource/be-vietnam-pro`. It covers Vietnamese diacritics well at small sizes. Numbers, code and JSON use **JetBrains Mono Variable** with `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums`.
- Scale (size / line height, px): `2xs` 11/16, `xs` 12/16, `sm` 13/20, `base` 14/20 (body default), `md` 16/24, `lg` 18/26, `xl` 22/28, `2xl` 28/34, `3xl` 36/42. Sizes `xl` and above use `-0.01em` letter spacing and weight 600.
- Section labels in panels: `2xs`, weight 600, uppercase, `0.06em` tracking, `text-3`. Metric values: mono `xl` weight 600. Captions: `xs` `text-3`.

### Space, radius, elevation, motion

- Spacing is a 4 px grid (Tailwind's default `--spacing: 0.25rem`). Used steps: 2, 4, 6, 8, 12, 16, 20, 24, 32, 40, 48, 64 px. Control height 32 px (compact rows 28 px, toolbar buttons 32 × 32). Panel padding 12 px; card padding 12–16 px.
- Radii: `--radius-xs` 4 px (chips inside dense rows), `--radius-sm` 6 px (inputs, small buttons), `--radius-md` 8 px (buttons, menus), `--radius-lg` 12 px (nodes, cards, popovers), `--radius-xl` 16 px (dialogs, floating run bar), full pill for status chips.
- Elevation, light: `--sd-shadow-1: 0 1px 2px rgb(27 30 34 / .06), 0 1px 1px rgb(27 30 34 / .04)`; `--sd-shadow-2: 0 4px 12px rgb(27 30 34 / .08), 0 1px 3px rgb(27 30 34 / .06)`; `--sd-shadow-3: 0 12px 32px rgb(27 30 34 / .14)`. Dark: `--sd-shadow-1: 0 1px 2px rgb(0 0 0 / .5)`; `--sd-shadow-2: 0 6px 16px rgb(0 0 0 / .45)`; `--sd-shadow-3: 0 16px 40px rgb(0 0 0 / .6)`, plus a 1 px `--sd-border` on every raised surface.
- Motion: `--sd-ease: cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.2,1)`; durations 120 ms (hover, press), 180 ms (popovers, panels), 260 ms (dialogs). `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` sets all transition durations to 0 (R19 covers playback).

### Layout and components

- **Top bar** (48 px, `surface`, bottom `border`). Left: our mark (three small rounded squares joined by two lines, drawn as inline SVG in `accent`) and the wordmark "SD Lab" in 15 px weight 700. Then the nav as text tabs (13 px, weight 500, `text-2`; the active tab is `text` with a 2 px `accent` underline 6 px below the text). Right: a search field look-alike button ("Search… ⌘K", 240 px, `surface-2`, radius md), the language switch (VI | EN segmented control), the theme switch (sun / moon / monitor segmented control), and the help menu (circle-help icon).
- **Lab grid** (below the top bar, fills the viewport): left palette 256 px, centre canvas, right inspector 320 px, bottom panel spanning canvas and inspector, default 260 px high, resizable from 160 px to 60% of the height through a 6 px drag handle (keyboard: focus the handle, then arrow keys move it by 16 px). Docked panels use `bg`; their content cards use `surface`.
- **Palette:** search input at the top (icon inside, 32 px). Categories as section labels. Items are 36 px rows: a 24 px icon tile (radius sm, category colour at 14% alpha behind the category-coloured icon), the component name (13 px weight 500), and a `plus` icon button that appears on hover or focus. Hover or focus shows a tooltip on the right with the description (max 280 px). Kinds a challenge does not allow are 50% opacity with a lock icon, and their tooltip says why.
- **Canvas:** `canvas` background with a 16 px dot grid (1 px dots, `grid`). Zoom controls bottom-left as a vertical pill group (plus, minus, fit, lock-grid). Minimap bottom-right, 168 × 112, radius lg, `surface` with 85% opacity, nodes drawn in their category colour. Edge legend top-left: a small card listing the four kinds as a 24 px line sample (colour and dash) and the name. Run bar top-centre (below).
- **Node card** (184 × 76): `surface`, 1 px `border`, radius lg, `shadow-1`. Row 1: 28 px icon tile, then the label (13 px weight 600, ellipsis) over the kind name (11 px `text-3`). Row 2, after a 1 px divider: a replicas chip ("× 2", mono 11 px, `surface-2`, radius xs) and the monthly cost ("$140 /mo", mono 11 px `text-2`) right-aligned. Left edge: the target handle (10 px circle, `surface` fill, 1.5 px `border-strong`), with one 6 px dot per accepted edge kind in that kind's colour, stacked above it. Right edge: the source handle. Selected: 2 px `accent` outline, 2 px offset. Locked: lock icon top-right in `text-3`, and the border becomes a dashed `border-strong`. Issues: a 16 px badge on the top-right corner, `over` for blocking and `busy` for warnings, with the count. Playback states: busy → `busy-soft` fill and 2 px `busy` border with a gauge icon; overloaded → `over-soft` fill and 2 px `over` border with a triangle icon and a slow 1.2 s pulse on the border (none under reduced motion); down → `down-soft` fill, `down` border, content at 55% opacity, power-off icon. Trace highlight: nodes on the path get an `accent-soft` 6 px halo; all others drop to 35% opacity.
- **Edges:** 2 px stroke in the kind colour with the kind's dash pattern, an arrowhead at the target, `smoothstep` with an 8 px corner radius. Selected: 3 px plus a 6 px `accent-soft` halo underneath. Edges with an issue get an issue badge at the midpoint.
- **Run bar** (floating, top-centre of the canvas, radius xl, `surface`, `shadow-2`, 40 px high): the Run button (accent, play icon, label "Run", hint "⌘↵"), a divider, the duration chip ("10 s"), and after a run, the playback controls: skip-to-start, play/pause, the scrub bar (360 px; 4 px track in `surface-3`, progress in `accent`, event ticks as 2 × 10 px marks coloured by severity, each focusable with a tooltip), the time readout (mono 12 px, "03.4 / 10.0 s"), the speed menu ("1×"), and skip-to-end. While a run is in progress, the Run button shows a spinner and the label "Running".
- **Inspector:** a header with the selection's icon tile and title (or "Design" when nothing is selected). Sections are Radix accordions with section-label headings. Fields are 32 px rows with the label on the left (13 px `text-2`) and the control on the right (max 160 px). Number fields are steppers: the value in mono, minus and plus icon buttons, and a unit suffix in `text-3`. A changed world parameter gets a 3 px `accent` bar on its left edge and a reset icon button. A locked node shows a notice row ("Locked by the challenge") and disabled controls.
- **Bottom panel:** tabs at the top-left (Metrics, Events, Logs, Simulation; 13 px weight 500, active tab underlined in `accent`), with a run selector (run history) at the top-right. **Metrics:** a row of stat tiles (`surface`, radius lg, label `xs` `text-3`, value mono `xl`, delta chip vs the previous run in `ok` or `over` depending on whether the change is better or worse for that metric), then the request breakdown as a stacked bar (ok, rejected, timed out, unreachable), then the per-node series chart (uPlot, 180 px high, series coloured `busy`/`accent`/`over` for busy/queued/rejected, axes in `text-3`, grid in `border`) with a node picker. **Events:** a table (time mono, node label, translated message), with the row matching the playback time highlighted. **Logs:** monospace lines with a level chip and a level filter. **Simulation:** the input as formatted JSON (mono 12 px, `surface`, scrollable), the engine version, and the buttons "Export run (JSON)" and "Copy input".
- **Dialogs** (save/open designs, import issues, shortcut sheet, connect target picker, about): radius xl, `surface`, `shadow-3`, `scrim` backdrop, 24 px padding, title `lg` weight 600, footer buttons right-aligned.
- **Buttons:** primary (accent fill, `on-accent` text), secondary (`surface`, 1 px `border-strong`), ghost (transparent, `surface-2` on hover), danger (`over` text, `over-soft` on hover). 32 px high, radius md, 13 px weight 500, 12 px horizontal padding, icon 16 px with 6 px gap.
- **Empty states:** a 40 px icon in `text-3` inside a 64 px circle of `surface-2`, one `md` title and one `sm` line. Examples: empty canvas ("Drag a component here, or press ⌘K"), no run yet, no saved designs.
- **Component icons** (lucide, 1.75 stroke): client `monitor-smartphone`, lb `split`, web-server `server`, postgres `database`; unknown kind `box`. Later kinds get an icon in the same table (`src/lab/canvas/icons.ts`).

## Phases and tasks

| Phase | Tasks | Ends with |
|---|---|---|
| 1. Scaffold: app, Docker, i18n, tokens, shell, worker, translations, CI | 1–9 | the app runs in the container, wasm loads in Chromium, CI green |
| 2. Canvas editing | 10–17 | build any design by mouse or keyboard, with live validation |
| 3. Workload, faults, world parameters, running, bottom panel | 18–24 | run a design and read metrics, events, logs, input |
| 4. Playback | 25–28 | dots, node states, scrub and speed, follow a request |
| 5. Designs, content entry points, palette, help | 29–33 | save/open/import/export, start from a challenge, ⌘K, tour |
| 6. Polish: a11y, e2e, visual and performance baselines | 34–37 | spec §12 done-when |

## Phase 1: Scaffold

### Task 1: `capabilities()` in sim-wasm and `Sim.capabilities()`

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/sim-wasm/src/lib.rs`, `packages/sim/src/binding.ts`, `packages/sim/src/sim.ts`, `packages/sim/src/types.ts`
- Create: `packages/sim/test/capabilities.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: wasm export `capabilities()`; `Sim.capabilities(): Capabilities`; type `Capabilities { components: string[]; faults: FaultKind[]; metrics: string[]; workloadParams: string[]; calibrationFields: string[]; configKeys: Record<string, string[]>; nodeOverrideKeys: string[] }` exported from `@sd/sim`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `packages/sim/test/capabilities.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { sim } from "../src/node";

describe("capabilities", () => {
  it("lists what this engine build supports", () => {
    const c = sim.capabilities();
    expect(c.components).toEqual(["client", "lb", "postgres", "web-server"]);
    // Enum order, as sim-core sorts them.
    expect(c.faults).toEqual(["traffic-surge-3x", "instance-down", "lb-node-down", "db-primary-down"]);
    expect(c.workloadParams).toEqual([]);
    expect(c.nodeOverrideKeys).toEqual(["costPerMonth", "rpsPerInstance", "serviceMs"]);
    expect(c.configKeys["web-server"]).toEqual(["cpu", "memoryGb"]);
    expect(c.configKeys["client"]).toEqual([]);
    expect(c.calibrationFields).toEqual(Object.keys(sim.defaultCalibration()).sort());
    expect(c.metrics).toContain("p95");
    expect(c.metrics).not.toContain("staleReads");
  });

  it("returns a fresh plain object each call", () => {
    const a = sim.capabilities();
    a.components.push("mutated");
    expect(sim.capabilities().components).not.toContain("mutated");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it and see it fail**

Run: `./dev pnpm --filter @sd/sim exec vitest run test/capabilities.test.ts`
(The `pretest` hook does not run with `exec`; if `packages/sim/wasm/` is missing, first run `./dev pnpm build:sim`.)
Expected: FAIL with `TypeError: sim.capabilities is not a function`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Export it from sim-wasm.** In `crates/sim-wasm/src/lib.rs`, add after `TraceSummary`:

```rust
/// `sim_core::Capabilities` as JS sees it. Kept here so sim-core needs no Serialize derive.
#[derive(Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
struct CapabilitiesJs<'a> {
    components: &'a [String],
    faults: &'a [sim_core::FaultKind],
    metrics: &'a [String],
    workload_params: &'a [String],
    calibration_fields: &'a [String],
    config_keys: &'a std::collections::BTreeMap<String, Vec<String>>,
    node_override_keys: &'a [String],
}
```

and at the end of the file:

```rust
/// What this engine build supports. The UI disables what is not built yet (fault kinds,
/// workload params) instead of letting a run fail.
#[wasm_bindgen]
pub fn capabilities() -> Result<JsValue, JsValue> {
    let c = sim_core::capabilities();
    to_js(&CapabilitiesJs {
        components: &c.components,
        faults: &c.faults,
        metrics: &c.metrics,
        workload_params: &c.workload_params,
        calibration_fields: &c.calibration_fields,
        config_keys: &c.config_keys,
        node_override_keys: &c.node_override_keys,
    })
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Expose it in TypeScript.**

`packages/sim/src/binding.ts`, in `WasmBinding` after `defaultCalibration(): unknown;`:

```ts
  capabilities(): unknown;
```

`packages/sim/src/types.ts`, append:

```ts
import type { FaultKind as FaultKindT } from "./generated/FaultKind";

/** What this engine build supports. Hand-written: sim-wasm serializes sim-core's struct. */
export interface Capabilities {
  /** Component kinds in the registry, sorted. */
  components: string[];
  /** Fault kinds the engine applies, in enum order. The others only emit `fault.unsupported`. */
  faults: FaultKindT[];
  /** Metric keys every run reports. The rest of `Metrics` stays null. */
  metrics: string[];
  /** `workload.params` keys the engine reads. */
  workloadParams: string[];
  /** `calibration` override keys. */
  calibrationFields: string[];
  /** Component kind -> its config keys. */
  configKeys: Record<string, string[]>;
  /** Config keys every node accepts as per-node overrides. */
  nodeOverrideKeys: string[];
}
```

`packages/sim/src/sim.ts`: add `Capabilities` to the type import, `capabilities(): Capabilities;` to `Sim` after `defaultCalibration`, and to `createSim`:

```ts
    capabilities: () => call(() => binding.capabilities()) as Capabilities,
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Run the tests and see them pass**

Run: `./dev pnpm --filter @sd/sim test`
Expected: all files pass, including `capabilities.test.ts (2 tests)`.

Run: `./dev pnpm test:rust`
Expected: fmt, tests and both clippy runs (native and `wasm32-unknown-unknown`) pass.

- [ ] **Step 6: Commit**

```bash
git add crates/sim-wasm/src/lib.rs packages/sim/src packages/sim/test/capabilities.test.ts
git commit -m "feat(sim-wasm): export capabilities() and Sim.capabilities()"
```

`apps/web/scripts/ensure-wasm.test.ts`:

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { isStale } from "./ensure-wasm.mjs";

function tmp(): string {
  return fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "ensure-wasm-"));
}
function touch(file: string, secondsAgo: number): void {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(file, "x");
  const t = Date.now() / 1000 - secondsAgo;
  fs.utimesSync(file, t, t);
}

describe("ensure-wasm", () => {
  it("is stale when an output is missing", () => {
    const d = tmp();
    touch(path.join(d, "src/lib.rs"), 100);
    expect(isStale([path.join(d, "out/a.wasm")], [path.join(d, "src")])).toBe(true);
  });

  it("is stale when any source is newer than the oldest output", () => {
    const d = tmp();
    touch(path.join(d, "out/a.wasm"), 50);
    touch(path.join(d, "out/b.wasm"), 10);
    touch(path.join(d, "src/deep/lib.rs"), 20);
    expect(isStale([path.join(d, "out/a.wasm"), path.join(d, "out/b.wasm")], [path.join(d, "src")])).toBe(true);
  });

  it("is fresh when every output is newer than every source", () => {
    const d = tmp();
    touch(path.join(d, "out/a.wasm"), 5);
    touch(path.join(d, "src/lib.rs"), 100);
    touch(path.join(d, "Cargo.lock"), 100);
    expect(isStale([path.join(d, "out/a.wasm")], [path.join(d, "src"), path.join(d, "Cargo.lock")])).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

Run: `./dev pnpm install && ./dev pnpm --filter @sd/web exec vitest run`
Expected: `toolchain.test.ts` passes; `ensure-wasm.test.ts` FAILS with `Failed to resolve import "./ensure-wasm.mjs"`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `apps/web/scripts/ensure-wasm.mjs`** (R3):

```js
// Builds packages/sim/wasm (node + web targets) when it is missing or older than the engine
// sources. Runs before `dev`, `build`, `test` and `typecheck` (plan ruling R3).
import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

const repo = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "../../..");
const OUTPUTS = ["packages/sim/wasm/web/sim_wasm_bg.wasm", "packages/sim/wasm/node/sim_wasm_bg.wasm"];
const SOURCES = [
  "crates/sim-core/src",
  "crates/sim-wasm/src",
  "crates/sim-core/Cargo.toml",
  "crates/sim-wasm/Cargo.toml",
  "Cargo.lock",
];

/** Newest modification time (ms) of a file, or of any file under a directory. */
export function newestMtime(p) {
  const st = fs.statSync(p);
  if (!st.isDirectory()) return st.mtimeMs;
  let max = 0;
  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(p)) max = Math.max(max, newestMtime(path.join(p, entry)));
  return max;
}

/** True when any output is missing or any source changed after the oldest output was built. */
export function isStale(outputs, sources) {
  if (!outputs.every((o) => fs.existsSync(o))) return true;
  const built = Math.min(...outputs.map((o) => fs.statSync(o).mtimeMs));
  return Math.max(...sources.filter((s) => fs.existsSync(s)).map(newestMtime)) > built;
}

if (process.argv[1] && import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href) {
  const outputs = OUTPUTS.map((p) => path.join(repo, p));
  const sources = SOURCES.map((p) => path.join(repo, p));
  if (isStale(outputs, sources)) {
    execFileSync("pnpm", ["--filter", "@sd/sim", "build:wasm"], { cwd: repo, stdio: "inherit" });
  } else {
    console.log("wasm is up to date");
  }
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import en from "../../messages/en.json";
import vi from "../../messages/vi.json";
import { flatten, icuArgs } from "./flatten";
import { SAME_IN_BOTH } from "./same-in-both";

const fv = flatten(vi);
const fe = flatten(en);

describe("message catalogs", () => {
  it("have the same keys in both languages", () => {
    expect(Object.keys(fv).filter((k) => !(k in fe))).toEqual([]);
    expect(Object.keys(fe).filter((k) => !(k in fv))).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("use the same ICU arguments in both languages", () => {
    const diff = Object.keys(fe)
      .filter((k) => k in fv)
      .filter((k) => icuArgs(fe[k]!).join() !== icuArgs(fv[k]!).join());
    expect(diff).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("have no empty values", () => {
    expect(Object.entries({ ...fv, ...fe }).filter(([, v]) => v.trim() === "").map(([k]) => k)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("do not leave English inside Vietnamese text", () => {
    const same = Object.keys(fv).filter((k) => fv[k] === fe[k] && fv[k]!.length > 12 && !SAME_IN_BOTH.has(k));
    expect(same).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("extracts ICU argument names, including plural arguments", () => {
    expect(icuArgs("{node}: {count, plural, one {# replica} other {# replicas}} left")).toEqual(["count", "node"]);
    expect(icuArgs("no arguments")).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Catalog helpers.**

`apps/web/src/i18n/flatten.ts`:

```ts
export type Messages = { [key: string]: string | Messages };

/** Nested catalog -> { "a.b.c": "text" }. */
export function flatten(messages: Messages, prefix = ""): Record<string, string> {
  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(messages)) {
    const key = prefix ? `${prefix}.${k}` : k;
    if (typeof v === "string") out[key] = v;
    else Object.assign(out, flatten(v, key));
  }
  return out;
}

/** Sorted, unique ICU argument names in a message (`{name}`, `{n, plural, …}`). */
export function icuArgs(text: string): string[] {
  const names = new Set<string>();
  for (const m of text.matchAll(/\{\s*([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*[,}]/g)) names.add(m[1]!);
  return [...names].sort();
}
```

`apps/web/src/i18n/same-in-both.ts`:

```ts
/** Keys whose text is legitimately identical in Vietnamese and English (names, units). */
export const SAME_IN_BOTH: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(["meta.title"]);
```

```ts
export const routing = defineRouting({
  locales: ["vi", "en"],
  defaultLocale: "vi",

});

export type Locale = (typeof routing.locales)[number];
```

```ts
import { hasLocale } from "next-intl";
import { getRequestConfig } from "next-intl/server";
import { routing } from "./routing";

export default getRequestConfig(async ({ requestLocale }) => {
  const requested = await requestLocale;
  const locale = hasLocale(routing.locales, requested) ? requested : routing.defaultLocale;
  return { locale, messages: (await import(`../../messages/${locale}.json`)).default };
});
```

`apps/web/app/[locale]/layout.tsx` (Task 5 adds the stylesheet and theme script):

```tsx
import { hasLocale, NextIntlClientProvider } from "next-intl";
import { getTranslations, setRequestLocale } from "next-intl/server";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { routing } from "@/i18n/routing";

type Props = { children: React.ReactNode; params: Promise<{ locale: string }> };

export function generateStaticParams() {
  return routing.locales.map((locale) => ({ locale }));
}

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: Omit<Props, "children">) {
  const { locale } = await params;
  const t = await getTranslations({ locale, namespace: "meta" });
  return { title: { default: t("title"), template: `%s · ${t("title")}` }, description: t("description") };
}

export default async function LocaleLayout({ children, params }: Props) {
  const { locale } = await params;
  if (!hasLocale(routing.locales, locale)) notFound();
  setRequestLocale(locale);
  return (
    <html lang={locale} suppressHydrationWarning>
      <body>
        <NextIntlClientProvider>{children}</NextIntlClientProvider>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}
```

```ts
import path from "node:path";

import { appRoot } from "../test/paths";

    }`;

  });

  });

  it("does not apply to tests", async () => {

  });
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Add the rule** to `apps/web/eslint.config.mjs` (R9):

```js
import i18next from "eslint-plugin-i18next";

// User-facing attributes; every other attribute is technical (class names, ids, roles…).
// The plugin anchors string patterns itself (^…$) and uses RegExp objects as given.
const TEXT_ATTRIBUTES = [
  "aria-label",
  "aria-description",
  "aria-roledescription",
  "aria-valuetext",
  "aria-placeholder",
  "title",
  "placeholder",
  "alt",
  "label",
];

export default [
  { ignores: [".next/**", "playwright-report/**", "test-results/**", "blob-report/**", "next-env.d.ts"] },
  ...compat.extends("next/core-web-vitals", "next/typescript"),
  {
    files: ["app/**/*.tsx", "src/**/*.tsx"],
    ignores: ["**/*.test.tsx"],
    plugins: { i18next },
    rules: {
      "i18next/no-literal-string": [
        "error",
        {
          mode: "jsx-only",
          "jsx-attributes": { include: TEXT_ATTRIBUTES, exclude: [".*"] },
          "jsx-components": { include: [], exclude: [] },
          // Pure punctuation/symbols/digits and ALL-CAPS tokens (HTTP, VI, EN) are not prose.
          // RegExp objects, because the plugin compiles strings without the `u` flag.
          words: { exclude: [/^[\s\d\p{P}\p{S}]+$/u, /^[A-Z0-9_-]+$/] },
        },
      ],
    },
  },
];
```

(The plugin merges options shallowly over its defaults, so these `jsx-attributes` and `words` lists replace the defaults rather than extend them. That is intended.)

Run: `./dev pnpm --filter @sd/web exec vitest run src/i18n/lint-rule.test.ts`
Expected: 4 passed.

`apps/web/src/ui/tokens.test.ts`:

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const css = fs.readFileSync(new URL("./tokens.css", import.meta.url), "utf8");

function block(selector: string): Record<string, string> {
  const start = css.indexOf(`${selector} {`);
  if (start < 0) throw new Error(`no block ${selector}`);
  const body = css.slice(start, css.indexOf("}", start));
  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const m of body.matchAll(/--sd-([a-z0-9-]+):\s*(#[0-9a-fA-F]{6});/g)) out[m[1]!] = m[2]!;
  return out;
}

function luminance(hex: string): number {
  const n = Number.parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16);
  const [r, g, b] = [n >> 16, (n >> 8) & 255, n & 255].map((v) => {
    const c = v / 255;
    return c <= 0.03928 ? c / 12.92 : ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4;
  }) as [number, number, number];
  return 0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b;
}

export function contrast(a: string, b: string): number {
  const [hi, lo] = [luminance(a), luminance(b)].sort((x, y) => y - x) as [number, number];
  return (hi + 0.05) / (lo + 0.05);
}

const THEMES = { light: block(":root"), dark: block('[data-theme="dark"]') };
const SURFACES = ["bg", "canvas", "surface", "surface-2", "surface-3"];

describe.each(Object.entries(THEMES))("%s theme tokens", (_name, t) => {
  const pair = (fg: string, bg: string, min: number) => {
    expect(t[fg], fg).toBeDefined();
    expect(t[bg], bg).toBeDefined();
    expect(contrast(t[fg]!, t[bg]!), `${fg} on ${bg}`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(min);
  };

  it("text is readable on every surface (4.5:1)", () => {
    for (const fg of ["text", "text-2", "text-3"]) for (const bg of SURFACES) pair(fg, bg, 4.5);
    pair("on-accent", "accent", 4.5);
    pair("accent", "surface", 4.5);
    for (const fg of ["ok", "busy", "over", "info"]) pair(fg, "surface", 4.5);
  });

  it("meaningful non-text colours stand out (3:1)", () => {
    const colours = ["down", "border-strong", "edge-http", "edge-db", "edge-cache", "edge-msg", "cat-general", "cat-compute", "cat-data"];
    for (const fg of colours) for (const bg of ["surface", "canvas"]) pair(fg, bg, 3);
    pair("busy", "busy-soft", 3);
    pair("over", "over-soft", 3);
    pair("down", "down-soft", 3);
    pair("focus", "bg", 3);
  });

  it("defines the same tokens in both themes", () => {
    expect(Object.keys(t).sort()).toEqual(Object.keys(THEMES.light).sort());
  });
});
```

```ts
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { readPref, resolveTheme, THEME_KEY, themeScript } from "./theme";

describe("theme", () => {
  it("resolves system against the OS setting", () => {
    expect(resolveTheme("system", true)).toBe("dark");
    expect(resolveTheme("system", false)).toBe("light");
    expect(resolveTheme("dark", false)).toBe("dark");
  });

    expect(readPref({ getItem: () => "dark" })).toBe("dark");

    new Function(themeScript)();
    expect(document.documentElement.dataset.theme).toBe("dark");
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Theme logic.**

`apps/web/src/shell/theme.ts`:

```ts
export type ThemePref = "light" | "dark" | "system";
export type Theme = "light" | "dark";
export const THEME_KEY = "sd-theme";
export const THEME_PREFS: readonly ThemePref[] = ["light", "dark", "system"];

export function resolveTheme(pref: ThemePref, systemDark: boolean): Theme {
  return pref === "system" ? (systemDark ? "dark" : "light") : pref;
}

export function readPref(storage: Pick<Storage, "getItem">): ThemePref {
  const v = storage.getItem(THEME_KEY);
  return v === "light" || v === "dark" || v === "system" ? v : "system";
}

/** Runs in <head> before first paint (plan ruling R26). Keep it dependency-free. */
export const themeScript = `(function(){try{var p=localStorage.getItem("${THEME_KEY}");if(p!=="light"&&p!=="dark")p="system";var d=p==="dark"||(p==="system"&&window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches);var r=document.documentElement;r.dataset.theme=d?"dark":"light";r.dataset.themePref=p;}catch(e){document.documentElement.dataset.theme="light";}})();`;

export function applyTheme(pref: ThemePref): void {
  const systemDark = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches;
  const root = document.documentElement;
  root.dataset.theme = resolveTheme(pref, systemDark);
  root.dataset.themePref = pref;
}
```

`apps/web/src/shell/use-theme.ts`:

```ts
"use client";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
import { applyTheme, readPref, THEME_KEY, type ThemePref } from "./theme";

const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
const notify = () => listeners.forEach((l) => l());

function subscribe(l: () => void) {
  listeners.add(l);
  return () => listeners.delete(l);
}
function getPref(): ThemePref {
  try {
    return readPref(localStorage);
  } catch {
    return "system";
  }
}

/** Current preference; follows OS changes live while the preference is "system". */
export function useTheme(): [ThemePref, (p: ThemePref) => void] {
  const pref = useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, getPref, () => "system" as ThemePref);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (pref !== "system") return;
    const mq = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)");
    const onChange = () => applyTheme("system");
    mq.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    return () => mq.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, [pref]);

  const set = useCallback((p: ThemePref) => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(THEME_KEY, p);
    } catch {
      /* storage unavailable: still apply for this page */
    }
    applyTheme(p);
    notify();
  }, []);

  return [pref, set];
}
```

`Button.tsx`:

```tsx
import { forwardRef } from "react";
import { cn } from "./cn";

type Props = React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & {
  variant?: "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost" | "danger";
  size?: "sm" | "md";
};

const VARIANTS = {
  primary: "bg-accent text-on-accent hover:bg-accent-hover",
  secondary: "bg-surface text-text border border-border-strong hover:bg-surface-2",
  ghost: "bg-transparent text-text-2 hover:bg-surface-2 hover:text-text",
  danger: "bg-transparent text-over hover:bg-over-soft",
} as const;

export const Button = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, Props>(function Button(
  { variant = "secondary", size = "md", className, type = "button", ...rest },
  ref,
) {
  return (
    <button
      ref={ref}
      type={type}
      className={cn(
        "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-md font-medium whitespace-nowrap",
        "transition-colors duration-120 ease-sd disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50",
        size === "md" ? "h-8 px-3 text-sm" : "h-7 px-2 text-xs",
        VARIANTS[variant],
        className,
      )}
      {...rest}
    />
  );
});
```

`IconButton.tsx` (the accessible name is required, and the tooltip shows the same text):

```tsx
import { forwardRef } from "react";
import { cn } from "./cn";
import { Tooltip } from "./Tooltip";

type Props = Omit<React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>, "aria-label"> & {
  label: string;
  shortcut?: string;
  active?: boolean;
};

export const IconButton = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, Props>(function IconButton(
  { label, shortcut, active, className, type = "button", children, ...rest },
  ref,
) {
  return (
    <Tooltip content={label} shortcut={shortcut}>
      <button
        ref={ref}
        type={type}
        aria-label={label}
        aria-pressed={active}
        className={cn(
          "inline-flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-md text-text-2",
          "transition-colors duration-120 ease-sd hover:bg-surface-2 hover:text-text",
          "disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50",
          active && "bg-accent-soft text-accent",
          className,
        )}
        {...rest}
      >
        {children}
      </button>
    </Tooltip>
  );
});
```

`Tooltip.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import * as T from "@radix-ui/react-tooltip";

export function TooltipProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return <T.Provider delayDuration={400} skipDelayDuration={200}>{children}</T.Provider>;
}

export function Tooltip({
  content,
  shortcut,
  side = "top",
  children,
}: {
  content: React.ReactNode;
  shortcut?: string;
  side?: "top" | "right" | "bottom" | "left";
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <T.Root>
      <T.Trigger asChild>{children}</T.Trigger>
      <T.Portal>
        <T.Content
          side={side}
          sideOffset={6}
          className="z-50 max-w-72 rounded-md border border-border bg-surface px-2 py-1 text-xs text-text shadow-2"
        >
          {content}
          {shortcut ? <kbd className="num ml-2 text-text-3">{shortcut}</kbd> : null}
        </T.Content>
      </T.Portal>
    </T.Root>
  );
}
```

```tsx
        </D.Content>
      </D.Portal>

}
```

`Segmented.tsx` (Radix ToggleGroup, single, never empty):

```tsx
"use client";
import * as TG from "@radix-ui/react-toggle-group";
import { cn } from "./cn";

export function Segmented<T extends string>({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  items,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: T;
  onChange: (v: T) => void;
  items: { value: T; label: string; icon?: React.ReactNode; title?: string }[];
}) {
  return (
    <TG.Root
      type="single"
      aria-label={label}
      value={value}
      onValueChange={(v) => v && onChange(v as T)}
      className="inline-flex h-8 items-center rounded-md border border-border bg-surface-2 p-0.5"
    >
      {items.map((it) => (
        <TG.Item
          key={it.value}
          value={it.value}
          aria-label={it.icon ? it.label : undefined}
          title={it.title ?? (it.icon ? it.label : undefined)}
          className={cn(
            "inline-flex h-7 min-w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-sm px-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-text-2",
            "data-[state=on]:bg-surface data-[state=on]:text-text data-[state=on]:shadow-1",
          )}
        >
          {it.icon ?? it.label}
        </TG.Item>
      ))}
    </TG.Root>
  );
}
```

`Banner.tsx`:

```tsx
import { AlertTriangle, Info, XCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "./cn";

const TONES = {
  info: { cls: "border-info/40 bg-surface text-text", Icon: Info, role: "status" },
  warn: { cls: "border-busy/50 bg-busy-soft text-text", Icon: AlertTriangle, role: "status" },
  error: { cls: "border-over/50 bg-over-soft text-text", Icon: XCircle, role: "alert" },
} as const;

export function Banner({
  tone,
  children,
  action,
}: {
  tone: keyof typeof TONES;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  action?: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  const { cls, Icon, role } = TONES[tone];
  return (
    <div role={role} className={cn("flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 text-sm", cls)}>
      <Icon size={16} aria-hidden className="shrink-0" />
      <div className="flex-1">{children}</div>
      {action}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/shell/NavLinks.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useTranslations } from "next-intl";
import { Link, usePathname } from "@/i18n/navigation";
import { cn } from "@/ui/cn";

const ITEMS = [
  { href: "/lab", key: "lab" },
  { href: "/learn", key: "learn" },
  { href: "/challenges", key: "challenges" },
  { href: "/leaderboard", key: "leaderboard" },
] as const;

export function NavLinks() {
  const t = useTranslations("nav");
  const pathname = usePathname();
  return (
    <nav aria-label={t("primary")} className="flex h-full items-stretch gap-1">
      {ITEMS.map((it) => {
        const current = pathname === it.href || pathname.startsWith(`${it.href}/`);
        return (
          <Link
            key={it.key}
            href={it.href}
            aria-current={current ? "page" : undefined}
            className={cn(
              "relative inline-flex items-center px-3 text-sm font-medium text-text-2 hover:text-text",
              current && "text-text after:absolute after:inset-x-3 after:bottom-[7px] after:h-0.5 after:rounded-full after:bg-accent",
            )}
          >
            {t(it.key)}
          </Link>
        );
      })}
    </nav>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/shell/LanguageSwitch.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useLocale, useTranslations } from "next-intl";
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "@/i18n/navigation";
import { routing, type Locale } from "@/i18n/routing";
import { Segmented } from "@/ui/Segmented";

export function LanguageSwitch() {
  const t = useTranslations("language");
  const locale = useLocale() as Locale;
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  return (
    <Segmented
      label={t("label")}
      value={locale}
      onChange={(next: Locale) => {
        // Remember the choice explicitly (R6); the middleware reads this cookie on "/".
        document.cookie = `NEXT_LOCALE=${next}; path=/; max-age=${60 * 60 * 24 * 365}; samesite=lax`;
        const query = window.location.search;
        router.replace(`${pathname}${query}`, { locale: next });
      }}
      items={routing.locales.map((l) => ({ value: l, label: l.toUpperCase(), title: t(l) }))}
    />
  );
}
```

(The visible text and accessible name are `VI` / `EN`; the full language name is the tooltip.)

`apps/web/src/shell/ThemeSwitch.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { Monitor, Moon, Sun } from "lucide-react";
import { useTranslations } from "next-intl";
import { Segmented } from "@/ui/Segmented";
import type { ThemePref } from "./theme";
import { useTheme } from "./use-theme";

export function ThemeSwitch() {
  const t = useTranslations("theme");
  const [pref, setPref] = useTheme();
  return (
    <Segmented<ThemePref>
      label={t("label")}
      value={pref}
      onChange={setPref}
      items={[
        { value: "light", label: t("light"), icon: <Sun size={14} aria-hidden /> },
        { value: "dark", label: t("dark"), icon: <Moon size={14} aria-hidden /> },
        { value: "system", label: t("system"), icon: <Monitor size={14} aria-hidden /> },
      ]}
    />
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/shell/Mark.tsx` (our own mark: three rounded squares joined by two lines):

```tsx
export function Mark({ size = 20 }: { size?: number }) {
  return (
    <svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden className="text-accent">
      <path d="M6 6 L14 10 L6 14" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.5" fill="none" />
      <rect x="2" y="3" width="6" height="6" rx="1.5" fill="currentColor" />
      <rect x="12" y="7" width="6" height="6" rx="1.5" fill="currentColor" />
      <rect x="2" y="11" width="6" height="6" rx="1.5" fill="currentColor" opacity="0.55" />
    </svg>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/shell/HelpMenu.tsx` (Radix DropdownMenu with a `CircleHelp` trigger `IconButton` labelled `t("help.label")`; items: `extraItems` first, then a separator and "About" which opens `AboutDialog`). `AboutDialog.tsx` shows `about.title`, `about.body` and `about.appVersion` with `version` from `process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_VERSION` (set in `next.config.ts` from `apps/web/package.json`'s `version`: `env: { NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_VERSION: pkg.version }`). Task 7 adds the engine version line.

`apps/web/src/shell/DesktopNotice.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useTranslations } from "next-intl";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Banner } from "@/ui/Banner";
import { Button } from "@/ui/Button";

const KEY = "sd-desktop-notice-dismissed";

export function DesktopNotice() {
  const t = useTranslations("shell");
  const [show, setShow] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const mq = window.matchMedia("(max-width: 1279px)");
    const update = () => setShow(mq.matches && sessionStorage.getItem(KEY) !== "1");
    update();
    mq.addEventListener("change", update);
    return () => mq.removeEventListener("change", update);
  }, []);
  if (!show) return null;
  return (
    <div className="px-3 pt-2">
      <Banner
        tone="info"
        action={
          <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => (sessionStorage.setItem(KEY, "1"), setShow(false))}>
            {t("dismiss")}
          </Button>
        }
      >
        {t("desktopNotice")}
      </Banner>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/shell/TopBar.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useTranslations } from "next-intl";
import { Link } from "@/i18n/navigation";
import { HelpMenu } from "./HelpMenu";
import { LanguageSwitch } from "./LanguageSwitch";
import { Mark } from "./Mark";
import { NavLinks } from "./NavLinks";
import { ThemeSwitch } from "./ThemeSwitch";

export function TopBar({ search, helpItems }: { search?: React.ReactNode; helpItems?: React.ReactNode }) {
  const t = useTranslations("nav");
  return (
    <header className="flex h-12 shrink-0 items-center gap-4 border-b border-border bg-surface px-3">
      <Link href="/lab" aria-label={t("home")} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-1">
        <Mark />
        <span className="text-[15px] font-bold tracking-tight">{t("wordmark")}</span>
      </Link>
      <NavLinks />
      <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2">
        {search}
        <LanguageSwitch />
        <ThemeSwitch />
        <HelpMenu extraItems={helpItems} />
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
```

(The wordmark is in the catalog as `nav.wordmark`, `"SD Lab"` in both languages. It is shorter than 13 characters, so it needs no `SAME_IN_BOTH` entry.)

```tsx
import { setRequestLocale } from "next-intl/server";
import { DesktopNotice } from "@/shell/DesktopNotice";
import { TopBar } from "@/shell/TopBar";
import { TooltipProvider } from "@/ui/Tooltip";

export default async function ShellLayout({
  children,
  params,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  params: Promise<{ locale: string }>;
}) {
  const { locale } = await params;
  setRequestLocale(locale);
  return (
    <TooltipProvider>
      <div className="flex h-dvh flex-col">
        <TopBar />
        <DesktopNotice />
        <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col">{children}</div>
      </div>
    </TooltipProvider>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";

test("the top bar reaches every section", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/en/lab");
  const nav = page.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Main" });
  for (const [name, path] of [["Learn", "/en/learn"], ["Challenges", "/en/challenges"], ["Leaderboard", "/en/leaderboard"], ["Lab", "/en/lab"]]) {
    await nav.getByRole("link", { name }).click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(new RegExp(`${path}$`));
    await expect(nav.getByRole("link", { name })).toHaveAttribute("aria-current", "page");
  }
});

test("switching language keeps the page and is remembered", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/en/challenges");
  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: "VI" }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/vi\/challenges$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText("Thử thách");
  await page.goto("/");
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/vi\/lab$/);
});

test("the theme choice survives a reload, and system follows the OS", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/en/lab");
  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: "Dark" }).click();
  await expect(page.locator("html")).toHaveAttribute("data-theme", "dark");
  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.locator("html")).toHaveAttribute("data-theme", "dark");

  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: "Follow the system" }).click();
  await page.emulateMedia({ colorScheme: "dark" });
  await expect(page.locator("html")).toHaveAttribute("data-theme", "dark");
  await page.emulateMedia({ colorScheme: "light" });
  await expect(page.locator("html")).toHaveAttribute("data-theme", "light");
});

test("narrow windows get a dismissible notice and still render", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 1100, height: 800 });
  await page.goto("/en/lab");
  await expect(page.getByText(/at least 1280 pixels/)).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Dismiss" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText(/at least 1280 pixels/)).toBeHidden();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("lab-page")).toBeVisible();
});
```

### Task 7: The engine in a Web Worker

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/engine/protocol.ts`, `apps/web/src/engine/worker-core.ts`, `apps/web/src/engine/worker-core.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/engine/test-fixtures.ts`, `apps/web/src/engine/sim.worker.ts`, `apps/web/src/engine/client.ts`, `apps/web/src/engine/client.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/engine/engine-store.ts`, `apps/web/src/engine/engine-store.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/engine/EngineProvider.tsx`, `apps/web/src/engine/EngineStatus.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lab/LabApp.tsx`, `apps/web/e2e/engine.spec.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/app/[locale]/(shell)/layout.tsx`, `apps/web/app/[locale]/(shell)/lab/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/shell/AboutDialog.tsx`, `apps/web/messages/{vi,en}.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Sim`, `SimRun`, `SimValidationError`, `SimInputError`, `Capabilities` (`@sd/sim`), `loadSim` (`@sd/sim/web`).
- Produces:
  - `EngineRequest`, `ResultOf`, `Envelope`, `Reply`, `EngineError`, `ValidateResult`, `RunResult` (`protocol.ts`).
  - `createHandler(sim, now?) -> (envelope) => { reply, transfer }`, `MAX_RUNS = 10` (`worker-core.ts`).
  - `EngineClient` with `request(req, transfer?)`, `onCrash(listener)`, `restart()`, `terminate()`; `EngineRequestError` (`.error: EngineError`); `EngineCrashError`; `createBrowserEngine()` (`client.ts`).
  - `createEngineStore(makeClient)` with state `{ status: "idle"|"loading"|"ready"|"error", info: EngineInfo|null, error: string|null, client }` and actions `start()`, `retry()` (`engine-store.ts`).
  - `EngineProvider`, `useEngineStore(selector)`, `useEngineClient()` (`EngineProvider.tsx`); `EngineStatus` (chip + error banner).

- [ ] **Step 1: Protocol types** `apps/web/src/engine/protocol.ts`:

```ts
import type {
  Calibration,
  CalibrationOverrides,
  Capabilities,
  ComponentMeta,
  Graph,
  GraphLimits,
  Metrics,
  SimEvent,
  SimInput,
  TimelineHeader,
  Trace,
  TraceSummary,
  ValidationIssue,
} from "@sd/sim";

/** Messages the main thread sends to the engine worker (spec §6). */
export type EngineRequest =
  | { type: "engineVersion" }
  | { type: "listComponents" }
  | { type: "defaultCalibration" }
  | { type: "capabilities" }
  | { type: "validate"; graph: Graph; limits: GraphLimits | null; calibration: CalibrationOverrides }
  | { type: "run"; input: SimInput }
  | { type: "trace"; runId: number; index: number }
  | { type: "resultJson"; runId: number }
  | { type: "dropRun"; runId: number };

export interface ValidateResult {
  issues: ValidationIssue[];
  /** Monthly cost per node id; null when the engine rejects the node's config (R10). */
  costs: Record<string, number | null>;
}

export interface RunResult {
  runId: number;
  metrics: Metrics;
  events: SimEvent[];
  timeline: { header: TimelineHeader; data: Float32Array };
  traces: TraceSummary[];
  /** Time the worker spent in simulate() and copying results out. */
  elapsedMs: number;
}

export interface ResultOf {
  engineVersion: string;
  listComponents: ComponentMeta[];
  defaultCalibration: Calibration;
  capabilities: Capabilities;
  validate: ValidateResult;
  run: RunResult;
  trace: Trace;
  resultJson: string;
  dropRun: null;
}

export type EngineError =
  | { kind: "validation"; issues: ValidationIssue[] }
  | { kind: "input"; message: string }
  | { kind: "load"; message: string }
  | { kind: "internal"; message: string };

export interface Envelope {
  id: number;
  request: EngineRequest;
}

export type Reply = { id: number; ok: true; value: unknown } | { id: number; ok: false; error: EngineError };
```

```ts
import type { Node, SimInput } from "@sd/sim";

const node = (id: string, kind: string, replicas = 1, config: Node["config"] = {}): Node => ({
  id,
  kind,
  label: "",
  replicas,
  config,
});

/** users -> lb -> web -> pg, all valid. */
export function fourTier(baseRps = 2000): SimInput {
  return {
    graph: {
      nodes: [
        node("users", "client"),
        node("lb", "lb", 2),
        node("web", "web-server", 3, { cpu: 2 }),
        node("pg", "postgres", 1, { readReplicas: 1 }),
      ],
      edges: [
        { id: "e1", from: "users", to: "lb", kind: "http" },
        { id: "e2", from: "lb", to: "web", kind: "http" },
        { id: "e3", from: "web", to: "pg", kind: "db" },
      ],
    },
    workload: { profile: "mixed", baseRps, writeRatio: 0.2, clients: 1000, clientTimeoutMs: 1000, surge: null, params: {} },
    faults: [],
    durationMs: 10_000,
    seed: 1,
  };
}
```

```ts
import { sim } from "@sd/sim/node";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { EngineRequest, ResultOf } from "./protocol";
import { fourTier } from "./test-fixtures";
import { createHandler, MAX_RUNS } from "./worker-core";

function setup() {
  const handle = createHandler(sim, () => 0);
  let id = 0;
  const call = <K extends EngineRequest["type"]>(request: Extract<EngineRequest, { type: K }>) => {
    const { reply, transfer } = handle({ id: ++id, request });
    return { reply, transfer, value: (reply.ok ? reply.value : undefined) as ResultOf[K] };
  };
  return { call };
}

describe("worker-core", () => {
  it("answers the info requests", () => {
    const { call } = setup();
    expect(call({ type: "engineVersion" }).value).toMatch(/^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/);
    expect(call({ type: "listComponents" }).value.map((m) => m.kind)).toContain("web-server");
    expect(call({ type: "defaultCalibration" }).value.webServiceMs).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(call({ type: "capabilities" }).value.faults).toContain("instance-down");
  });

  it("validates and prices every node; the costs add up to the run's cost", () => {
    const { call } = setup();
    const input = fourTier();
    const v = call({ type: "validate", graph: input.graph, limits: null, calibration: {} }).value;
    expect(v.issues.filter((i) => i.blocking)).toEqual([]);
    expect(v.costs["users"]).toBe(0);
    const sum = Object.values(v.costs).reduce<number>((a, c) => a + (c ?? 0), 0);
    const run = call({ type: "run", input }).value;
    expect(sum).toBeCloseTo(run.metrics.costPerMonth, 6);
  });

  it("uses the design's calibration for prices", () => {
    const { call } = setup();
    const g = fourTier().graph;
    const base = call({ type: "validate", graph: g, limits: null, calibration: {} }).value.costs["pg"]!;
    const doubled = call({ type: "validate", graph: g, limits: null, calibration: { pgCostPerInstance: 1000 } }).value.costs["pg"]!;
    expect(doubled).not.toBe(base);
  });

  it("prices a node with a rejected config as null", () => {
    const { call } = setup();
    const g = fourTier().graph;
    g.nodes[2]!.config = { cpu: 999 };
    const v = call({ type: "validate", graph: g, limits: null, calibration: {} }).value;
    expect(v.costs["web"]).toBeNull();
    expect(v.issues.map((i) => i.code)).toContain("config.invalid");
  });

  it("applies challenge limits", () => {
    const { call } = setup();
    const g = fourTier().graph;
    const v = call({ type: "validate", graph: g, limits: { maxNodes: 3, maxInstances: null, allowedKinds: null }, calibration: {} }).value;
    expect(v.issues.map((i) => i.code)).toContain("limits.too-many-nodes");
  });

  it("runs and transfers the timeline buffer", () => {
    const { call } = setup();
    const { value, transfer } = call({ type: "run", input: fourTier() });
    expect(value.runId).toBe(1);
    expect(value.timeline.header.frames).toBe(100);
    expect(value.timeline.header.fields).toEqual(["inFlight", "queued", "busy", "rejected", "up"]);
    expect(value.timeline.data.length).toBe(100 * value.timeline.header.nodeIds.length * 5);
    expect(transfer).toEqual([value.timeline.data.buffer]);
    expect(value.traces.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(value.elapsedMs).toBe(0);
  });

  it("serves traces and JSON of kept runs, and frees runs beyond the last 10", () => {
    const { call } = setup();
    for (let i = 0; i < MAX_RUNS + 1; i++) call({ type: "run", input: fourTier(500) });
    const first = call({ type: "trace", runId: 1, index: 0 }).reply;
    expect(first).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: { kind: "internal", message: "unknown run 1" } });
    const last = call({ type: "trace", runId: MAX_RUNS + 1, index: 0 }).value;
    expect(last.steps[0]?.nodeId).toBe("users");
    expect(JSON.parse(call({ type: "resultJson", runId: MAX_RUNS + 1 }).value).metrics).toBeDefined();
    expect(call({ type: "dropRun", runId: MAX_RUNS + 1 }).reply.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(call({ type: "trace", runId: MAX_RUNS + 1, index: 0 }).reply.ok).toBe(false);
  });

  it("turns engine errors into typed replies", () => {
    const { call } = setup();
    const bad = fourTier();
    bad.graph.nodes = bad.graph.nodes.filter((n) => n.kind !== "client");
    bad.graph.edges = bad.graph.edges.filter((e) => e.from !== "users");
    expect(call({ type: "run", input: bad }).reply).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: { kind: "validation" } });

    const nonsense = { ...fourTier(), durationMs: "soon" } as unknown as ReturnType<typeof fourTier>;
    expect(call({ type: "run", input: nonsense }).reply).toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: { kind: "input" } });
  });
});
```
