- [ ] **Step 4: The handler** `apps/web/src/engine/worker-core.ts`:

```ts
import {
  SimInputError,
  SimValidationError,
  type CalibrationOverrides,
  type Node,
  type Sim,
  type SimInput,
  type SimRun,
} from "@sd/sim";
import type { EngineError, EngineRequest, Envelope, Reply, RunResult, ValidateResult } from "./protocol";

/** Runs whose handles the worker keeps for trace(i) and JSON export (R18). */
export const MAX_RUNS = 10;
const COST_CLIENT = "__cost-client";
const COST_EDGE = "__cost-edge";

export interface Handled {
  reply: Reply;
  transfer: Transferable[];
}

function toEngineError(e: unknown): EngineError {
  if (e instanceof SimValidationError) return { kind: "validation", issues: e.issues };
  if (e instanceof SimInputError) return { kind: "input", message: e.message };
  return { kind: "internal", message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) };
}

export function createHandler(sim: Sim, now: () => number = () => performance.now()) {
  const runs = new Map<number, SimRun>();
  let nextRunId = 1;
  const metas = sim.listComponents();
  const metaByKind = new Map(metas.map((m) => [m.kind, m]));
  const clientKind = metas.find((m) => m.role === "client")?.kind ?? "client";

  /** Plan ruling R10: a 100 ms, zero-traffic run of client -> node prices the node alone. */
  function nodeCost(node: Node, calibration: CalibrationOverrides): number | null {
    const meta = metaByKind.get(node.kind);
    if (!meta) return null;
    const kind = meta.accepts[0];
    if (meta.role === "client" || kind === undefined) return 0;
    const input: SimInput = {
      graph: {
        nodes: [{ id: COST_CLIENT, kind: clientKind, label: "", replicas: 1, config: {} }, node],
        edges: [{ id: COST_EDGE, from: COST_CLIENT, to: node.id, kind }],
      },
      workload: { profile: "mixed", baseRps: 0, writeRatio: 0, clients: 1, clientTimeoutMs: 1000, surge: null, params: {} },
      faults: [],
      durationMs: 100,
      seed: 0,
      calibration,
    };
    try {
      const run = sim.simulate(input);
      try {
        return run.metrics.costPerMonth;
      } finally {
        run.free();
      }
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  function kept(runId: number): SimRun {
    const run = runs.get(runId);
    if (!run) throw new Error(`unknown run ${runId}`);
    return run;
  }

  function dispatch(r: EngineRequest): [unknown, Transferable[]] {
    switch (r.type) {
      case "engineVersion":
        return [sim.engineVersion(), []];
      case "listComponents":
        return [metas, []];
      case "defaultCalibration":
        return [sim.defaultCalibration(), []];
      case "capabilities":
        return [sim.capabilities(), []];
      case "validate": {
        const issues = sim.validateGraph(r.graph, r.limits ?? undefined);
        const costs: Record<string, number | null> = {};
        for (const n of r.graph.nodes) costs[n.id] = nodeCost(n, r.calibration);
        return [{ issues, costs } satisfies ValidateResult, []];
      }
      case "run": {
        const t0 = now();
        const run = sim.simulate(r.input);
        const tl = run.timeline;
        const result: RunResult = {
          runId: nextRunId++,
          metrics: run.metrics,
          events: run.events,
          timeline: {
            header: { frameMs: tl.frameMs, frames: tl.frames, nodeIds: [...tl.nodeIds], fields: [...tl.fields] },
            // A JS-owned copy (sim-wasm copies it out of wasm memory); transferred, never read again here.
            data: tl.data,
          },
          traces: run.traceSummaries(),
          elapsedMs: 0,
        };
        result.elapsedMs = now() - t0;
        runs.set(result.runId, run);
        while (runs.size > MAX_RUNS) {
          const [oldestId, oldest] = runs.entries().next().value as [number, SimRun];
          oldest.free();
          runs.delete(oldestId);
        }
        return [result, [result.timeline.data.buffer]];
      }
      case "trace":
        return [kept(r.runId).trace(r.index), []];
      case "resultJson":
        return [kept(r.runId).resultJson(), []];
      case "dropRun": {
        runs.get(r.runId)?.free();
        runs.delete(r.runId);
        return [null, []];
      }
    }
  }

  return function handle({ id, request }: Envelope): Handled {
    try {
      const [value, transfer] = dispatch(request);
      return { reply: { id, ok: true, value }, transfer };
    } catch (e) {
      return { reply: { id, ok: false, error: toEngineError(e) }, transfer: [] };
    }
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The worker entry** `apps/web/src/engine/sim.worker.ts`:

```ts
// Engine worker: owns the wasm instance (spec §6). Loaded by client.ts with
// new Worker(new URL("./sim.worker.ts", import.meta.url), { type: "module" }).
import { loadSim } from "@sd/sim/web";
import type { Envelope, Reply } from "./protocol";
import { createHandler } from "./worker-core";

type Scope = {
  onmessage: ((e: MessageEvent<Envelope>) => void) | null;
  postMessage(message: Reply, transfer: Transferable[]): void;
};
const scope = self as unknown as Scope;

// loadSim() without a URL: the wasm-bindgen glue resolves sim_wasm_bg.wasm with
// new URL(..., import.meta.url), which webpack emits as a hashed asset (plan ruling R2).
const ready = loadSim().then((sim) => createHandler(sim));

scope.onmessage = async (e) => {
  let handle: Awaited<typeof ready>;
  try {
    handle = await ready;
  } catch (err) {
    scope.postMessage({ id: e.data.id, ok: false, error: { kind: "load", message: String(err) } }, []);
    return;
  }
  const { reply, transfer } = handle(e.data);
  scope.postMessage(reply, transfer);
};
```

```ts
import { sim } from "@sd/sim/node";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { EngineClient, EngineCrashError, EngineRequestError, type WorkerLike } from "./client";
import type { Envelope, Reply } from "./protocol";
import { fourTier } from "./test-fixtures";
import { createHandler } from "./worker-core";

class FakeWorker implements WorkerLike {
  onmessage: ((e: MessageEvent<Reply>) => void) | null = null;
  onerror: ((e: ErrorEvent) => void) | null = null;
  terminated = false;
  private handle = createHandler(sim, () => 0);
  postMessage(msg: Envelope) {
    queueMicrotask(() => {
      if (this.terminated) return;
      const { reply } = this.handle(msg);
      this.onmessage?.({ data: reply } as MessageEvent<Reply>);
    });
  }
  terminate() {
    this.terminated = true;
  }
  crash(message: string) {
    this.onerror?.({ message, preventDefault() {} } as ErrorEvent);
  }
}

describe("EngineClient", () => {
  it("matches replies to requests", async () => {
    const client = new EngineClient(() => new FakeWorker());
    const [version, metas] = await Promise.all([
      client.request({ type: "engineVersion" }),
      client.request({ type: "listComponents" }),
    ]);
    expect(version).toMatch(/^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/);
    expect(metas.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it("rejects with the engine's typed error", async () => {
    const client = new EngineClient(() => new FakeWorker());
    const bad = fourTier();
    bad.graph.nodes = bad.graph.nodes.filter((n) => n.id !== "users");
    bad.graph.edges = bad.graph.edges.filter((e) => e.from !== "users");
    const err = await client.request({ type: "run", input: bad }).catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(EngineRequestError);
    expect((err as EngineRequestError).error.kind).toBe("validation");
  });

  it("rejects pending requests and notifies listeners when the worker crashes", async () => {
    let worker!: FakeWorker;
    const client = new EngineClient(() => (worker = new FakeWorker()));
    const onCrash = vi.fn();
    client.onCrash(onCrash);
    worker.terminated = true; // never answers
    const pending = client.request({ type: "engineVersion" });
    worker.crash("boom");
    await expect(pending).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(EngineCrashError);
    expect(onCrash).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it("restart spawns a fresh worker that answers", async () => {
    const factory = vi.fn(() => new FakeWorker());
    const client = new EngineClient(factory);
    client.restart();
    expect(factory).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    await expect(client.request({ type: "engineVersion" })).resolves.toMatch(/\d/);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 7: The client** `apps/web/src/engine/client.ts`:

```ts
import type { EngineError, EngineRequest, Envelope, Reply, ResultOf } from "./protocol";

export interface WorkerLike {
  onmessage: ((e: MessageEvent<Reply>) => void) | null;
  onerror: ((e: ErrorEvent) => void) | null;
  postMessage(message: Envelope, transfer?: Transferable[]): void;
  terminate(): void;
}

/** The worker answered with an error (validation issues, bad input, wasm load failure). */
export class EngineRequestError extends Error {
  constructor(readonly error: EngineError) {
    super(error.kind === "validation" ? "validation" : error.message);
    this.name = "EngineRequestError";
  }
}

/** The worker died or was restarted while the request was pending. */
export class EngineCrashError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "EngineCrashError";
  }
}

type Pending = { resolve: (v: unknown) => void; reject: (e: Error) => void };

export class EngineClient {
  #factory: () => WorkerLike;
  #worker!: WorkerLike;
  #next = 1;
  #pending = new Map<number, Pending>();
  #listeners = new Set<(e: EngineCrashError) => void>();

  constructor(factory: () => WorkerLike) {
    this.#factory = factory;
    this.#spawn();
  }

  #spawn(): void {
    const w = this.#factory();
    w.onmessage = (e) => {
      const r = e.data;
      const p = this.#pending.get(r.id);
      if (!p) return;
      this.#pending.delete(r.id);
      if (r.ok) p.resolve(r.value);
      else p.reject(new EngineRequestError(r.error));
    };
    w.onerror = (e) => {
      e.preventDefault?.();
      this.#fail(new EngineCrashError(e.message || "engine worker error"), true);
    };
    this.#worker = w;
  }

  #fail(err: EngineCrashError, notify: boolean): void {
    for (const p of this.#pending.values()) p.reject(err);
    this.#pending.clear();
    if (notify) for (const l of this.#listeners) l(err);
  }

  request<K extends EngineRequest["type"]>(
    request: Extract<EngineRequest, { type: K }>,
    transfer: Transferable[] = [],
  ): Promise<ResultOf[K]> {
    const id = this.#next++;
    return new Promise<ResultOf[K]>((resolve, reject) => {
      this.#pending.set(id, { resolve: resolve as (v: unknown) => void, reject });
      this.#worker.postMessage({ id, request }, transfer);
    });
  }

  onCrash(listener: (e: EngineCrashError) => void): () => void {
    this.#listeners.add(listener);
    return () => this.#listeners.delete(listener);
  }

  /** Kills the worker (pending requests reject) and starts a fresh one. */
  restart(): void {
    this.#worker.terminate();
    this.#fail(new EngineCrashError("engine restarted"), false);
    this.#spawn();
  }

  terminate(): void {
    this.#worker.terminate();
    this.#fail(new EngineCrashError("engine stopped"), false);
  }
}

export function createBrowserEngine(): EngineClient {
  // This exact `new Worker(new URL(...))` shape is what webpack recognizes as a worker entry.
  return new EngineClient(
    () => new Worker(new URL("./sim.worker.ts", import.meta.url), { type: "module" }) as unknown as WorkerLike,
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Engine store, provider, status UI.**

`apps/web/src/engine/engine-store.ts`:

```ts
import type { Calibration, Capabilities, ComponentMeta } from "@sd/sim";
import { createStore } from "zustand/vanilla";
import { EngineRequestError, type EngineClient } from "./client";

export interface EngineInfo {
  version: string;
  components: ComponentMeta[];
  calibration: Calibration;
  capabilities: Capabilities;
}

export interface EngineState {
  status: "idle" | "loading" | "ready" | "error";
  info: EngineInfo | null;
  error: string | null;
  client: EngineClient | null;
  start(): void;
  retry(): void;
}

export function createEngineStore(makeClient: () => EngineClient) {
  return createStore<EngineState>()((set, get) => {
    async function load(client: EngineClient) {
      set({ status: "loading", error: null });
      try {
        const [version, components, calibration, capabilities] = await Promise.all([
          client.request({ type: "engineVersion" }),
          client.request({ type: "listComponents" }),
          client.request({ type: "defaultCalibration" }),
          client.request({ type: "capabilities" }),
        ]);
        set({ status: "ready", info: { version, components, calibration, capabilities } });
      } catch (e) {
        const message = e instanceof EngineRequestError ? e.error.kind : e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
        set({ status: "error", error: message });
      }
    }
    return {
      status: "idle",
      info: null,
      error: null,
      client: null,
      start() {
        if (get().client) return;
        const client = makeClient();
        client.onCrash((e) => set({ status: "error", error: e.message }));
        set({ client });
        void load(client);
      },
      retry() {
        const client = get().client;
        if (!client) return get().start();
        client.restart();
        void load(client);
      },
    };
  });
}

export type EngineStore = ReturnType<typeof createEngineStore>;
```

`apps/web/src/engine/engine-store.test.ts`: with the `FakeWorker` from `client.test.ts` (move it to `test-fixtures.ts` as `FakeWorker`, exported, so both tests share it), check that `start()` goes `loading → ready` and fills `info.components`, that a factory whose worker answers every request with `{ ok: false, error: { kind: "load", message: "x" } }` ends in `status: "error"`, and that `retry()` after that, with a working worker, ends in `ready`.

`apps/web/src/engine/EngineProvider.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useStore } from "zustand";
import { createBrowserEngine } from "./client";
import { createEngineStore, type EngineState, type EngineStore } from "./engine-store";

const Ctx = createContext<EngineStore | null>(null);

/** One engine per tab, created on first use (the shell mounts it; the Lab starts it). */
export function EngineProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [store] = useState(() => createEngineStore(createBrowserEngine));
  useEffect(() => () => store.getState().client?.terminate(), [store]);
  return <Ctx.Provider value={store}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
}

export function useEngineStore<T>(selector: (s: EngineState) => T): T {
  const store = useContext(Ctx);
  if (!store) throw new Error("useEngineStore outside EngineProvider");
  return useStore(store, selector);
}

/** Starts the engine when the calling component mounts. */
export function useEngineStart(): void {
  const start = useEngineStore((s) => s.start);
  useEffect(() => start(), [start]);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Browser smoke test** `apps/web/e2e/engine.spec.ts` (Review Focus 1):

```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";

const WASM = /\/_next\/static\/media\/sim_wasm_bg\.[0-9a-f]+\.wasm$/;

test("the engine loads in a worker from a hashed wasm asset", async ({ page }) => {
  const errors: string[] = [];
  page.on("console", (m) => m.type() === "error" && errors.push(m.text()));
  page.on("pageerror", (e) => errors.push(String(e)));
  const wasm = page.waitForResponse((r) => WASM.test(new URL(r.url()).pathname));
  await page.goto("/en/lab");
  const res = await wasm;
  expect(res.status()).toBe(200);
  expect(res.headers()["content-type"]).toBe("application/wasm");
  expect(res.headers()["cache-control"]).toContain("immutable");
  const status = page.getByTestId("engine-status");
  await expect(status).toHaveAttribute("data-status", "ready");
  await expect(status).toContainText(/\d+\.\d+\.\d+/);
  expect(errors).toEqual([]);
});

test("a failed wasm load shows a retry banner, and retry recovers", async ({ page, context }) => {
  let block = true;
  await context.route(WASM, (route) => (block ? route.abort() : route.continue()));
  await page.goto("/en/lab");
  await expect(page.getByRole("alert")).toContainText("could not start");
  block = false;
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Try again" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("engine-status")).toHaveAttribute("data-status", "ready");
});
```

Run: `./dev sh -c 'pnpm --filter @sd/web build && pnpm --filter @sd/web test:e2e e2e/engine.spec.ts'`
Expected: `2 passed`.

If the first test fails because no request matches `WASM` (a 404 for `sim_wasm_bg.wasm` next to the worker chunk, or `data-status="error"`), webpack did not emit the asset. Apply the R2 fallback:
1. In `ensure-wasm.mjs`, after the freshness check, copy `packages/sim/wasm/web/sim_wasm_bg.wasm` to `apps/web/public/engine/sim_wasm_bg.<first 12 hex of its sha256>.wasm`, delete older copies there, and write `apps/web/src/engine/wasm-url.ts` containing `export const WASM_URL = "/engine/sim_wasm_bg.<hash>.wasm";`.
2. In `sim.worker.ts`, use `loadSim(WASM_URL)`.
3. In `next.config.ts`, add `headers()` that sets `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable` and `Content-Type: application/wasm` for `/engine/:file*`.
4. Change `WASM` in this test to `/^\/engine\/sim_wasm_bg\.[0-9a-f]{12}\.wasm$/`, and record the switch in the commit message.

If the second test cannot intercept the worker's request (some Playwright builds do not route worker fetches), block the worker script instead with `/\/_next\/static\/.*sim\.worker.*\.js$/`. That exercises the crash path, which shows the same banner. Note the change in the commit.

```ts
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { repoRoot } from "../test/paths";
import { EVENT_CODES, VALIDATION_CODES } from "./engine-codes";

/** Non-test Rust source under crates/sim-core/src (everything before #[cfg(test)]). */
function engineSource(): string {
  const out: string[] = [];
  const walk = (dir: string) => {
    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
      const p = path.join(dir, e.name);
      if (e.isDirectory()) walk(p);
      else if (e.name.endsWith(".rs")) out.push(fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8").split("#[cfg(test)]")[0]!);
    }
  };
  walk(path.join(repoRoot, "crates/sim-core/src"));
  return out.join("\n");
}

const sorted = (s: Iterable<string>) => [...new Set(s)].sort();

describe("engine code registry", () => {
  const src = engineSource();

  it("lists exactly the event codes the engine emits", () => {
    const found = [
      ...[...src.matchAll(/event\([^;]*?"([a-z]+\.[a-z0-9-]+)"/g)].map((m) => m[1]!),
      ...[...src.matchAll(/code:\s*"([a-z]+\.[a-z0-9-]+)"/g)].map((m) => m[1]!),
    ];
    expect(found.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(sorted(EVENT_CODES)).toEqual(sorted(found));
  });

  it("lists exactly the validation codes in sim-core's codes module", () => {
    const mod = src.slice(src.indexOf("pub mod codes {"));
    const body = mod.slice(0, mod.indexOf("}"));
    const found = [...body.matchAll(/pub const [A-Z_]+: &str = "([^"]+)";/g)].map((m) => m[1]!);
    expect(found.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(sorted(VALIDATION_CODES)).toEqual(sorted(found));
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: The registry** `apps/web/src/i18n/engine-codes.ts`:

```ts
import type {
  Arrivals,
  Calibration,
  Capabilities,
  ComponentMeta,
  EdgeKind,
  FaultKind,
  Metrics,
  Profile,
  TraceAction,
  TraceOutcome,
} from "@sd/sim";

/** Every SimEvent.code sim-core emits (checked against the source by engine-codes.test.ts). */
export const EVENT_CODES = [
  "db.no-replica",
  "db.primary-down",
  "db.promoted",
  "fault.no-target",
  "fault.traffic-surge",
  "fault.unsupported",
  "instance.down",
  "lb.node-down",
  "node.overloaded",
] as const;

/** Every ValidationIssue.code (sim-core validate::codes). */
export const VALIDATION_CODES = [
  "calibration.invalid",
  "client.no-path",
  "config.invalid",
  "edge.not-accepted",
  "edge.unknown-node",
  "graph.cycle",
  "graph.no-client",
  "limits.kind-not-allowed",
  "limits.too-many-instances",
  "limits.too-many-nodes",
  "node.duplicate-id",
  "node.unknown-kind",
  "node.unreachable",
  "workload.invalid",
] as const;

/** Compile-time check that a tuple names every member of a union. */
type Exhaustive<U, T extends readonly U[]> = [Exclude<U, T[number]>] extends [never] ? T : never;
const exhaustive =
  <U,>() =>
  <const T extends readonly U[]>(t: Exhaustive<U, T>) =>
    t;

export const ALL_FAULT_KINDS = exhaustive<FaultKind>()([
  "traffic-surge-3x",
  "instance-down",
  "instance-slow",
  "lb-node-down",
  "cache-down",
  "db-overloaded",
  "db-primary-down",
  "db-replica-down",
  "db-stalled",
  "broker-down",
  "object-storage-down",
  "kv-node-down",
  "third-party-down",
  "third-party-slow",
]);

export const METRIC_KEYS = exhaustive<keyof Metrics>()([
  "p50", "p95", "p99", "availability", "throughputRps", "costPerMonth", "spofCount", "exposedBackends",
  "peakBusy", "peakDbConnections", "cacheOps", "requestsTotal", "requestsOk", "requestsRejected",
  "requestsTimedOut", "requestsUnreachable", "duplicateIds", "outOfOrderIds", "lostUpdates",
  "writeConflicts", "duplicateWrites", "staleReads", "peakQueueDepth", "lostMessages", "statusFlaps",
  "staleOnline", "inboxWritesPerSec",
]);

export const PROFILES = exhaustive<Profile>()(["read-heavy", "write-heavy", "mixed", "chat", "feed", "jobs"]);
export const ARRIVALS = exhaustive<Arrivals>()(["poisson", "uniform"]);
export const EDGE_KINDS = exhaustive<EdgeKind>()(["http", "db", "cache", "msg"]);
export const TRACE_OUTCOMES = exhaustive<TraceOutcome>()(["delivered", "rejected", "timeout", "unreachable"]);
export const TRACE_ACTIONS = exhaustive<TraceAction>()(["send", "serve", "reject", "down"]);
/** sim-core TIMELINE_FIELDS, in order. */
export const TIMELINE_FIELDS = ["inFlight", "queued", "busy", "rejected", "up"] as const;

/** Catalog keys the UI needs for everything the engine can produce (plan ruling R8). */
export function requiredKeys(metas: ComponentMeta[], caps: Capabilities, calibration: Calibration): string[] {
  const keys = new Set<string>();
  const add = (k: string) => keys.add(k);
  EVENT_CODES.forEach((c) => add(`engine.event.${c}`));
  VALIDATION_CODES.forEach((c) => add(`engine.validation.${c}`));
  ALL_FAULT_KINDS.forEach((k) => add(`fault.${k}`));
  METRIC_KEYS.forEach((k) => add(`metric.${k}`));
  PROFILES.forEach((k) => add(`profile.${k}`));
  ARRIVALS.forEach((k) => add(`arrivals.${k}`));
  EDGE_KINDS.forEach((k) => add(`edgeKind.${k}`));
  TRACE_OUTCOMES.forEach((k) => add(`traceOutcome.${k}`));
  TRACE_ACTIONS.forEach((k) => add(`traceAction.${k}`));
  TIMELINE_FIELDS.forEach((k) => add(`timelineField.${k}`));
  for (const m of metas) {
    add(m.labelKey);
    add(`componentDescription.${m.kind}`);
    add(`category.${m.category}`);
    add(`role.${m.role}`);
    for (const f of m.fields) {
      add(`configField.${f.key}`);
      f.options.forEach((o) => add(`configOption.${f.key}.${o}`));
    }
  }
  caps.nodeOverrideKeys.forEach((k) => add(`override.${k}`));
  caps.workloadParams.forEach((k) => add(`workloadParam.${k}`));
  Object.keys(calibration).forEach((k) => add(`calibration.${k}`));
  return [...keys].sort();
}
```

### Task 9: Web CI workflow, cached image build, root scripts (Phase 1 gate)

**Files:**
- Create: `.github/workflows/web.yml`, `compose.ci.yaml`
- Modify: `.github/workflows/engine.yml`, `package.json`, `README.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: root scripts `test:web` (lint, typecheck, build, e2e of `@sd/web`) and `dev:web`; the `web` workflow.

- [ ] **Step 1: Root scripts** in `package.json`:

```json
    "dev:web": "pnpm --filter @sd/web dev",
    "test:web": "pnpm --filter @sd/web lint && pnpm --filter @sd/web typecheck && pnpm --filter @sd/web build && pnpm --filter @sd/web test:e2e",
```

(`pnpm test` already runs `@sd/web`'s unit tests through `pnpm -r test`, after `@sd/sim`, because pnpm runs workspace scripts in dependency order.)

- [ ] **Step 2: CI cache mounts** `compose.ci.yaml` (used only in CI with `COMPOSE_FILE=compose.yaml:compose.ci.yaml`; compose merges volumes by their container path, so these replace the named volumes):

```yaml
# CI only: host directories over the caches, so actions/cache can save and restore them.
services:
  dev:
    volumes:
      - ${CI_CACHE:?set CI_CACHE}/pnpm-store:/pnpm-store
      - ${CI_CACHE:?set CI_CACHE}/cargo-registry:/usr/local/cargo/registry
      - ${CI_CACHE:?set CI_CACHE}/cargo-target:/work/target
      - ${CI_CACHE:?set CI_CACHE}/next:/work/apps/web/.next
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `.github/workflows/web.yml`** (R28):

```yaml
name: web

# Spec §9 path filters, plus the build files (a dependency or image change can break the app).
on:
  pull_request:
    paths: &paths
      - "apps/web/**"
      - "packages/**"
      - "crates/sim-wasm/**"
      - "crates/sim-core/**"
      - "content/**"
      - "Dockerfile"
      - "compose.yaml"
      - "compose.ci.yaml"
      - "package.json"
      - "pnpm-lock.yaml"
      - "pnpm-workspace.yaml"
      - ".github/workflows/web.yml"
  push:
    branches: [main]
    paths: *paths

concurrency:
  group: web-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  web:
    name: lint, typecheck, unit, build, e2e + axe (dev container)
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 45
    env:
      COMPOSE_FILE: compose.yaml:compose.ci.yaml
      CI_CACHE: ${{ github.workspace }}/.ci-cache
      CI: "true"
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      # The dev image, with Playwright's Chromium in a cached layer (plan ruling R4).
      - uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v3
      - name: Build dev image (layer cache)
        uses: docker/build-push-action@v6
        with:
          context: .
          load: true
          tags: system-design-dev:2
          cache-from: type=gha,scope=dev-image
          cache-to: type=gha,mode=max,scope=dev-image

      - name: Restore pnpm store
        uses: actions/cache@v4
        with:
          path: .ci-cache/pnpm-store
          key: pnpm-${{ hashFiles('pnpm-lock.yaml') }}
          restore-keys: pnpm-

      - name: Restore cargo registry and wasm build
        uses: actions/cache@v4
        with:
          path: |
            .ci-cache/cargo-registry
            .ci-cache/cargo-target
          key: cargo-web-${{ hashFiles('Cargo.lock') }}-${{ hashFiles('crates/sim-core/**', 'crates/sim-wasm/**') }}
          restore-keys: cargo-web-${{ hashFiles('Cargo.lock') }}-

      - name: Restore Next build cache
        uses: actions/cache@v4
        with:
          path: .ci-cache/next/cache
          key: next-${{ hashFiles('pnpm-lock.yaml') }}-${{ hashFiles('apps/web/**', 'packages/**') }}
          restore-keys: next-${{ hashFiles('pnpm-lock.yaml') }}-

      - name: Install JS dependencies
        run: ./dev pnpm install --frozen-lockfile

      - name: Build wasm
        run: ./dev pnpm --filter @sd/web ensure-wasm

      - name: Lint
        run: ./dev pnpm --filter @sd/web lint

      - name: Typecheck
        run: ./dev pnpm --filter @sd/web typecheck

      - name: Unit tests
        run: ./dev pnpm --filter @sd/web test

      - name: Next build
        run: ./dev pnpm --filter @sd/web build

      - name: E2E and accessibility (Playwright)
        run: ./dev pnpm --filter @sd/web test:e2e

      - name: Upload Playwright report
        if: failure()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: playwright-report
          path: apps/web/playwright-report
          retention-days: 7
```

(If the runner's actionlint or GitHub rejects the YAML anchor `&paths`, write both lists out in full; GitHub Actions has supported anchors since 2025, but an older actionlint in a pre-commit hook may not.)

### Task 10: Design model, pure edit operations, store with undo/redo

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/lab/model/design.ts`, `apps/web/src/lab/model/ids.ts`, `apps/web/src/lab/model/edge-kind.ts`, `apps/web/src/lab/model/ops.ts`, `apps/web/src/lab/model/ops.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/lab/store/design-store.ts`, `apps/web/src/lab/store/design-store.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/lab/model/test-metas.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - Types `Design`, `LabNode`, `LabEdge`, `LabGroup`, `ChallengeRef`, `XY`; `DESIGN_FORMAT_VERSION = 1`; `NODE_SIZE = { width: 184, height: 76 }`; `emptyDesign(name?)`, `defaultWorkload()` (`design.ts`).
  - `nextId(prefix, taken)`, `nodeNumber(id, kind)` (`ids.ts`); `inferEdgeKind(meta) -> EdgeKind | null` (`edge-kind.ts`).
  - Pure ops in `ops.ts`. Each returns the **same object** when nothing changes:
    - `addNode(d, meta, at) -> { design, id }`, `moveNodes(d, moves)`, `connect(d, from, to, metas) -> ConnectResult`, `deleteItems(d, sel)`, `updateNode(d, id, { label?, replicas? })`, `setConfig(d, id, key, value | undefined)`, `setEdgeKind(d, id, kind)`;
    - `copyItems(d, nodeIds) -> Clipboard`, `pasteItems(d, clip, offset) -> { design, ids }`, `duplicateItems(d, ids)`;
    - `groupNodes(d, ids) -> { design, id } | null`, `ungroup(d, id)`, `moveGroup(d, id, to)`, `renameGroup(d, id, label)`, `fitGroups(d)`;
    - `setName`, `setWorkload(d, patch)`, `setDuration`, `setSeed`, `setFaults`, `setCalibration(d, key, value | undefined, defaults)`;
    - `isLocked(d, id)`, `GRID = 16`, `snap(v)`.
  - `createDesignStore(initial?)`: Zustand vanilla store with state `{ design, past, future, selection, clipboard }` and actions `apply(fn)`, `replace(design)`, `undo()`, `redo()`, `select(partial)`, `clearSelection()`, `setClipboard(clip)`; `HISTORY_LIMIT = 200`; `Selection = { nodes: string[]; edges: string[]; groups: string[] }`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Model types** `apps/web/src/lab/model/design.ts`:

```ts
import type { CalibrationOverrides, ConfigValue, EdgeKind, Fault, GraphLimits, Workload } from "@sd/sim";

export const DESIGN_FORMAT_VERSION = 1 as const;
/** Layout size of a node card (R14). */
export const NODE_SIZE = { width: 184, height: 76 } as const;

export interface XY {
  x: number;
  y: number;
}

export interface LabNode {
  id: string;
  kind: string;
  /** "" means "show the translated component name plus its number" (R12). */
  label: string;
  replicas: number;
  config: Record<string, ConfigValue>;
  /** Absolute canvas position (R12), even for group members. */
  position: XY;
  groupId: string | null;
}

export interface LabEdge {
  id: string;
  from: string;
  to: string;
  kind: EdgeKind;
}

/** UI-only frame; never sent to the engine (R15). */
export interface LabGroup {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  position: XY;
  size: { width: number; height: number };
}

export interface ChallengeRef {
  id: string;
  mode: string;
  limits: GraphLimits;
}

export interface Design {
  designFormatVersion: typeof DESIGN_FORMAT_VERSION;
  name: string;
  nodes: LabNode[];
  edges: LabEdge[];
  groups: LabGroup[];
  workload: Workload;
  faults: Fault[];
  /** Only the world parameters the user changed (R11, Review Focus 4). */
  calibration: CalibrationOverrides;
  durationMs: number;
  seed: number;
  /** Node ids a challenge locks (R12). */
  locked: string[];
  challenge: ChallengeRef | null;
}

export function defaultWorkload(): Workload {
  return { profile: "mixed", baseRps: 1000, writeRatio: 0.2, clients: 1000, clientTimeoutMs: 1000, surge: null, params: {} };
}

export function emptyDesign(name = ""): Design {
  return {
    designFormatVersion: DESIGN_FORMAT_VERSION,
    name,
    nodes: [],
    edges: [],
    groups: [],
    workload: defaultWorkload(),
    faults: [],
    calibration: {},
    durationMs: 10_000,
    seed: 1,
    locked: [],
    challenge: null,
  };
}
```

`apps/web/src/lab/model/ids.ts`:

```ts
/** Lowest `${prefix}${n}` (n >= 1) not in `taken`. */
export function nextId(prefix: string, taken: Iterable<string>): string {
  const set = new Set(taken);
  for (let n = 1; ; n++) {
    const id = `${prefix}${n}`;
    if (!set.has(id)) return id;
  }
}

/** The number in an id made by nextId(`${kind}-`, …), or null for any other id. */
export function nodeNumber(id: string, kind: string): number | null {
  if (!id.startsWith(`${kind}-`)) return null;
  const rest = id.slice(kind.length + 1);
  return /^[1-9]\d*$/.test(rest) ? Number(rest) : null;
}
```

`apps/web/src/lab/model/edge-kind.ts`:

```ts
import type { ComponentMeta, EdgeKind } from "@sd/sim";

/** R13 / spec §5.1: database targets get `db`, everything else `http`; null when the target accepts nothing. */
export function inferEdgeKind(target: ComponentMeta): EdgeKind | null {
  if (target.accepts.length === 0) return null;
  return target.role === "database" ? "db" : "http";
}
```

`apps/web/src/lab/model/test-metas.ts` (tests only):

```ts
import { sim } from "@sd/sim/node";
import type { ComponentMeta } from "@sd/sim";

export const METAS: ComponentMeta[] = sim.listComponents();
export const META_BY_KIND: ReadonlyMap<string, ComponentMeta> = new Map(METAS.map((m) => [m.kind, m]));
export const meta = (kind: string): ComponentMeta => {
  const m = META_BY_KIND.get(kind);
  if (!m) throw new Error(`no component ${kind}`);
  return m;
};
```

```ts
  updateNode,
} from "./ops";
import { META_BY_KIND, meta } from "./test-metas";

function build(): Design {
  let d = emptyDesign();
  for (const [kind, x] of [["client", 0], ["lb", 240], ["web-server", 480], ["postgres", 720]] as const) {
    d = addNode(d, meta(kind), { x, y: 0 }).design;
  }
  return d;
}

describe("design ops", () => {
  it("numbers new nodes per kind and reuses the lowest free number", () => {
    let d = emptyDesign();
    const a = addNode(d, meta("web-server"), { x: 3, y: 9 });
    const b = addNode(a.design, meta("web-server"), { x: 0, y: 0 });
    expect([a.id, b.id]).toEqual(["web-server-1", "web-server-2"]);
    expect(a.design.nodes[0]!.position).toEqual({ x: 0, y: 16 }); // snapped to 16
    d = deleteItems(b.design, { nodes: ["web-server-1"], edges: [], groups: [] });
    expect(addNode(d, meta("web-server"), { x: 0, y: 0 }).id).toBe("web-server-1");
    expect(nodeNumber("web-server-12", "web-server")).toBe(12);
    expect(nodeNumber("custom", "web-server")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("connects with the inferred kind and refuses bad connections", () => {
    const d = build();
    const r1 = connect(d, "client-1", "lb-1", META_BY_KIND);
    expect("edgeId" in r1 && r1.design.edges[0]).toEqual({ id: "e1", from: "client-1", to: "lb-1", kind: "http" });
    const r2 = connect(d, "web-server-1", "postgres-1", META_BY_KIND);
    expect("edgeId" in r2 && r2.design.edges[0]!.kind).toBe("db");
    expect(connect(d, "lb-1", "client-1", META_BY_KIND)).toMatchObject({ refused: "not-accepted", design: d });
    expect(connect(d, "lb-1", "lb-1", META_BY_KIND)).toMatchObject({ refused: "self" });
    const once = (r1 as { design: Design }).design;
    expect(connect(once, "client-1", "lb-1", META_BY_KIND)).toMatchObject({ refused: "duplicate", design: once });
  });

  it("deletes nodes with their edges but never a locked node", () => {
    let d = build();
    for (const [a, b] of [["client-1", "lb-1"], ["lb-1", "web-server-1"], ["web-server-1", "postgres-1"]]) {
      d = (connect(d, a!, b!, META_BY_KIND) as { design: Design }).design;
    }
    d = { ...d, locked: ["client-1"] };
    const after = deleteItems(d, { nodes: ["client-1", "web-server-1"], edges: [], groups: [] });
    expect(after.nodes.map((n) => n.id)).toEqual(["client-1", "lb-1", "postgres-1"]);
    expect(after.edges.map((e) => e.id)).toEqual(["e1"]);
    // An edge touching a locked node can still be deleted (R12).
    expect(deleteItems(after, { nodes: [], edges: ["e1"], groups: [] }).edges).toEqual([]);
    expect(deleteItems(after, { nodes: ["client-1"], edges: [], groups: [] })).toBe(after);
  });

  it("leaves locked nodes' label, replicas and config alone, but lets them move", () => {
    const d = { ...build(), locked: ["web-server-1"] };
    expect(updateNode(d, "web-server-1", { replicas: 3 })).toBe(d);
    expect(setConfig(d, "web-server-1", "cpu", 4)).toBe(d);
    const moved = moveNodes(d, { "web-server-1": { x: 496, y: 32 } });
    expect(moved.nodes.find((n) => n.id === "web-server-1")!.position).toEqual({ x: 496, y: 32 });
  });

  it("clamps replicas to 1..1000 whole numbers and removes unset config keys", () => {
    let d = build();
    d = updateNode(d, "web-server-1", { replicas: 2.7 });
    expect(d.nodes.find((n) => n.id === "web-server-1")!.replicas).toBe(3);
    expect(updateNode(d, "web-server-1", { replicas: 0 }).nodes.find((n) => n.id === "web-server-1")!.replicas).toBe(1);
    d = setConfig(d, "web-server-1", "cpu", 4);
    expect(d.nodes.find((n) => n.id === "web-server-1")!.config).toEqual({ cpu: 4 });
    d = setConfig(d, "web-server-1", "cpu", undefined);
    expect(d.nodes.find((n) => n.id === "web-server-1")!.config).toEqual({});
  });

  it("duplicates nodes with the edges between them, offset by 32 px", () => {
    let d = build();
    d = (connect(d, "lb-1", "web-server-1", META_BY_KIND) as { design: Design }).design;
    d = (connect(d, "web-server-1", "postgres-1", META_BY_KIND) as { design: Design }).design;
    const { design, ids } = duplicateItems(d, ["lb-1", "web-server-1"]);
    expect(ids).toEqual(["lb-2", "web-server-2"]);
    expect(design.edges.at(-1)).toEqual({ id: "e3", from: "lb-2", to: "web-server-2", kind: "http" });
    expect(design.nodes.find((n) => n.id === "lb-2")!.position).toEqual({ x: 272, y: 32 });
  });

  it("pastes a clipboard into another design", () => {
    const clip = copyItems(build(), ["postgres-1"]);
    const { design, ids } = pasteItems(emptyDesign(), clip, { x: 0, y: 0 });
    expect(ids).toEqual(["postgres-1"]);
    expect(design.nodes[0]!.groupId).toBeNull();
  });

  it("groups, moves the group with its members, and ungroups", () => {
    const g = groupNodes(build(), ["lb-1", "web-server-1"])!;
    expect(g.id).toBe("g1");
    const frame = g.design.groups[0]!;
    expect(frame.position).toEqual({ x: 240 - 32, y: 0 - 32 - 24 });
    expect(frame.size).toEqual({ width: 480 + 184 - 240 + 64, height: 76 + 64 + 24 });
    const moved = moveGroup(g.design, "g1", { x: frame.position.x + 100, y: frame.position.y + 50 });
    expect(moved.nodes.find((n) => n.id === "lb-1")!.position).toEqual({ x: 340, y: 50 });
    expect(moved.nodes.find((n) => n.id === "client-1")!.position).toEqual({ x: 0, y: 0 });
    const flat = ungroup(moved, "g1");
    expect(flat.groups).toEqual([]);
    expect(flat.nodes.every((n) => n.groupId === null)).toBe(true);
    expect(flat.nodes.find((n) => n.id === "lb-1")!.position).toEqual({ x: 340, y: 50 });
  });

  it("stores only changed world parameters", () => {
    const defaults = { webServiceMs: 5 } as never;
    let d = setCalibration(emptyDesign(), "webServiceMs", 8, defaults);
    expect(d.calibration).toEqual({ webServiceMs: 8 });
    d = setCalibration(d, "webServiceMs", 5, defaults);
    expect(d.calibration).toEqual({});
  });
});
```

```ts
import type { Calibration, CalibrationOverrides, ComponentMeta, ConfigValue, EdgeKind, Fault, Workload } from "@sd/sim";
import { NODE_SIZE, type Design, type LabEdge, type LabGroup, type LabNode, type XY } from "./design";
import { inferEdgeKind } from "./edge-kind";
import { nextId } from "./ids";

export const GRID = 16;
export const MAX_REPLICAS = 1000;
const GROUP_PAD = 32;
const GROUP_LABEL = 24;

export const snap = (v: number): number => Math.round(v / GRID) * GRID;
export const isLocked = (d: Design, id: string): boolean => d.locked.includes(id);

export interface Clipboard {
  nodes: LabNode[];
  edges: LabEdge[];
}
export interface ItemSelection {
  nodes: readonly string[];
  edges: readonly string[];
  groups: readonly string[];
}

export function addNode(d: Design, meta: ComponentMeta, at: XY): { design: Design; id: string } {
  const id = nextId(`${meta.kind}-`, d.nodes.map((n) => n.id));
  const node: LabNode = {
    id,
    kind: meta.kind,
    label: "",
    replicas: 1,
    config: {},
    position: { x: snap(at.x), y: snap(at.y) },
    groupId: null,
  };
  return { design: { ...d, nodes: [...d.nodes, node] }, id };
}

export function moveNodes(d: Design, moves: Readonly<Record<string, XY>>): Design {
  let changed = false;
  const nodes = d.nodes.map((n) => {
    const p = moves[n.id];
    if (!p || (p.x === n.position.x && p.y === n.position.y)) return n;
    changed = true;
    return { ...n, position: { x: p.x, y: p.y } };
  });
  return changed ? { ...d, nodes } : d;
}

export type ConnectRefusal = "self" | "duplicate" | "not-accepted" | "unknown-node";
export type ConnectResult = { design: Design; edgeId: string } | { design: Design; refused: ConnectRefusal };

export function connect(d: Design, from: string, to: string, metas: ReadonlyMap<string, ComponentMeta>): ConnectResult {
  if (from === to) return { design: d, refused: "self" };
  const source = d.nodes.find((n) => n.id === from);
  const target = d.nodes.find((n) => n.id === to);
  if (!source || !target) return { design: d, refused: "unknown-node" };
  if (d.edges.some((e) => e.from === from && e.to === to)) return { design: d, refused: "duplicate" };
  const meta = metas.get(target.kind);
  // An unknown kind (from an imported file) gets http; validation reports node.unknown-kind.
  const kind = meta ? inferEdgeKind(meta) : "http";
  if (kind === null) return { design: d, refused: "not-accepted" };
  const id = nextId("e", d.edges.map((e) => e.id));
  return { design: { ...d, edges: [...d.edges, { id, from, to, kind }] }, edgeId: id };
}

export function deleteItems(d: Design, sel: ItemSelection): Design {
  const nodeIds = new Set(sel.nodes.filter((id) => !isLocked(d, id)));
  const edgeIds = new Set(sel.edges);
  const groupIds = new Set(sel.groups);
  const nodes = d.nodes
    .filter((n) => !nodeIds.has(n.id))
    .map((n) => (n.groupId !== null && groupIds.has(n.groupId) ? { ...n, groupId: null } : n));
  const edges = d.edges.filter((e) => !edgeIds.has(e.id) && !nodeIds.has(e.from) && !nodeIds.has(e.to));
  const groups = d.groups.filter((g) => !groupIds.has(g.id));
  if (nodes.length === d.nodes.length && edges.length === d.edges.length && groups.length === d.groups.length) return d;
  return dropEmptyGroups({ ...d, nodes, edges, groups });
}

function mapNode(d: Design, id: string, f: (n: LabNode) => LabNode): Design {
  if (isLocked(d, id)) return d;
  let changed = false;
  const nodes = d.nodes.map((n) => {
    if (n.id !== id) return n;
    const next = f(n);
    changed = next !== n;
    return next;
  });
  return changed ? { ...d, nodes } : d;
}

export function updateNode(d: Design, id: string, patch: { label?: string; replicas?: number }): Design {
  return mapNode(d, id, (n) => {
    const label = patch.label ?? n.label;
    const replicas =
      patch.replicas === undefined
        ? n.replicas
        : Math.min(MAX_REPLICAS, Math.max(1, Math.round(Number.isFinite(patch.replicas) ? patch.replicas : 1)));
    return label === n.label && replicas === n.replicas ? n : { ...n, label, replicas };
  });
}

export function setConfig(d: Design, id: string, key: string, value: ConfigValue | undefined): Design {
  return mapNode(d, id, (n) => {
    if (n.config[key] === value) return n;
    const config = { ...n.config };
    if (value === undefined) delete config[key];
    else config[key] = value;
    return { ...n, config };
  });
}

export function setEdgeKind(d: Design, id: string, kind: EdgeKind): Design {
  const e = d.edges.find((x) => x.id === id);
  if (!e || e.kind === kind) return d;
  return { ...d, edges: d.edges.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, kind } : x)) };
}

export function copyItems(d: Design, nodeIds: readonly string[]): Clipboard {
  const set = new Set(nodeIds);
  return {
    nodes: d.nodes.filter((n) => set.has(n.id)).map((n) => structuredClone(n)),
    edges: d.edges.filter((e) => set.has(e.from) && set.has(e.to)).map((e) => ({ ...e })),
  };
}

export function pasteItems(d: Design, clip: Clipboard, offset: XY): { design: Design; ids: string[] } {
  const taken = new Set(d.nodes.map((n) => n.id));
  const idMap = new Map<string, string>();
  const nodes = clip.nodes.map((n) => {
    const id = nextId(`${n.kind}-`, taken);
    taken.add(id);
    idMap.set(n.id, id);
    return {
      ...structuredClone(n),
      id,
      position: { x: n.position.x + offset.x, y: n.position.y + offset.y },
      groupId: null,
    };
  });
  const edgeTaken = new Set(d.edges.map((e) => e.id));
  const edges = clip.edges.map((e) => {
    const id = nextId("e", edgeTaken);
    edgeTaken.add(id);
    return { ...e, id, from: idMap.get(e.from)!, to: idMap.get(e.to)! };
  });
  if (nodes.length === 0) return { design: d, ids: [] };
  return { design: { ...d, nodes: [...d.nodes, ...nodes], edges: [...d.edges, ...edges] }, ids: nodes.map((n) => n.id) };
}

export function duplicateItems(d: Design, ids: readonly string[]): { design: Design; ids: string[] } {
  return pasteItems(d, copyItems(d, ids), { x: 2 * GRID, y: 2 * GRID });
}

function frameAround(members: readonly LabNode[]): Pick<LabGroup, "position" | "size"> {
  const minX = Math.min(...members.map((n) => n.position.x)) - GROUP_PAD;
  const minY = Math.min(...members.map((n) => n.position.y)) - GROUP_PAD - GROUP_LABEL;
  const maxX = Math.max(...members.map((n) => n.position.x + NODE_SIZE.width)) + GROUP_PAD;
  const maxY = Math.max(...members.map((n) => n.position.y + NODE_SIZE.height)) + GROUP_PAD;
  return { position: { x: minX, y: minY }, size: { width: maxX - minX, height: maxY - minY } };
}

function dropEmptyGroups(d: Design): Design {
  const used = new Set(d.nodes.map((n) => n.groupId));
  const groups = d.groups.filter((g) => used.has(g.id));
  return groups.length === d.groups.length ? d : { ...d, groups };
}

export function groupNodes(d: Design, ids: readonly string[]): { design: Design; id: string } | null {
  const set = new Set(ids);
  const members = d.nodes.filter((n) => set.has(n.id));
  if (members.length === 0) return null;
  const id = nextId("g", d.groups.map((g) => g.id));
  const group: LabGroup = { id, label: "", ...frameAround(members) };
  const nodes = d.nodes.map((n) => (set.has(n.id) ? { ...n, groupId: id } : n));
  return { design: dropEmptyGroups({ ...d, nodes, groups: [...d.groups, group] }), id };
}

export function ungroup(d: Design, id: string): Design {
  if (!d.groups.some((g) => g.id === id)) return d;
  return {
    ...d,
    groups: d.groups.filter((g) => g.id !== id),
    nodes: d.nodes.map((n) => (n.groupId === id ? { ...n, groupId: null } : n)),
  };
}

export function moveGroup(d: Design, id: string, to: XY): Design {
  const g = d.groups.find((x) => x.id === id);
  if (!g) return d;
  const dx = to.x - g.position.x;
  const dy = to.y - g.position.y;
  if (dx === 0 && dy === 0) return d;
  return {
    ...d,
    groups: d.groups.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, position: { x: to.x, y: to.y } } : x)),
    nodes: d.nodes.map((n) =>
      n.groupId === id ? { ...n, position: { x: n.position.x + dx, y: n.position.y + dy } } : n,
    ),
  };
}

export function renameGroup(d: Design, id: string, label: string): Design {
  const g = d.groups.find((x) => x.id === id);
  if (!g || g.label === label) return d;
  return { ...d, groups: d.groups.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, label } : x)) };
}

/** Refits every frame around its members (after auto-layout). */
export function fitGroups(d: Design): Design {
  if (d.groups.length === 0) return d;
  return {
    ...d,
    groups: d.groups.map((g) => {
      const members = d.nodes.filter((n) => n.groupId === g.id);
      return members.length ? { ...g, ...frameAround(members) } : g;
    }),
  };
}

export const setName = (d: Design, name: string): Design => (d.name === name ? d : { ...d, name });
export const setDuration = (d: Design, durationMs: number): Design =>
  d.durationMs === durationMs ? d : { ...d, durationMs };
export const setSeed = (d: Design, seed: number): Design => (d.seed === seed ? d : { ...d, seed });
export const setFaults = (d: Design, faults: Fault[]): Design => ({ ...d, faults });

export function setWorkload(d: Design, patch: Partial<Workload>): Design {
  const keys = Object.keys(patch) as (keyof Workload)[];
  if (keys.every((k) => Object.is(d.workload[k], patch[k]))) return d;
  return { ...d, workload: { ...d.workload, ...patch } };
}

export function setCalibration(
  d: Design,
  key: keyof Calibration,
  value: number | undefined,
  defaults: Calibration,
): Design {
  const calibration: CalibrationOverrides = { ...d.calibration };
  if (value === undefined || value === defaults[key]) delete calibration[key];
  else calibration[key] = value;
  const same =
    Object.keys(calibration).length === Object.keys(d.calibration).length &&
    (Object.keys(calibration) as (keyof Calibration)[]).every((k) => calibration[k] === d.calibration[k]);
  return same ? d : { ...d, calibration };
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { emptyDesign } from "../model/design";
import { addNode, moveNodes } from "../model/ops";
import { meta } from "../model/test-metas";
import { createDesignStore, HISTORY_LIMIT } from "./design-store";

describe("design store", () => {
  it("records one step per change and restores exact snapshots", () => {
    const s = createDesignStore();
    const d0 = s.getState().design;
    s.getState().apply((d) => addNode(d, meta("client"), { x: 0, y: 0 }).design);
    const d1 = s.getState().design;
    s.getState().apply((d) => moveNodes(d, { "client-1": { x: 64, y: 64 } })); // a whole drag is one apply
    expect(s.getState().past).toHaveLength(2);
    s.getState().undo();
    expect(s.getState().design).toBe(d1);
    s.getState().undo();
    expect(s.getState().design).toBe(d0);
    s.getState().redo();
    expect(s.getState().design).toBe(d1);
  });

  it("ignores no-op changes and clears redo on a new change", () => {
    const s = createDesignStore();
    s.getState().apply((d) => d);
    expect(s.getState().past).toHaveLength(0);
    s.getState().apply((d) => addNode(d, meta("client"), { x: 0, y: 0 }).design);
    s.getState().undo();
    s.getState().apply((d) => addNode(d, meta("lb"), { x: 0, y: 0 }).design);
    expect(s.getState().future).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it(`keeps the last ${HISTORY_LIMIT} steps`, () => {
    const s = createDesignStore();
    for (let i = 0; i < HISTORY_LIMIT + 50; i++) s.getState().apply((d) => ({ ...d, seed: i + 2 }));
    expect(s.getState().past).toHaveLength(HISTORY_LIMIT);
    for (let i = 0; i < HISTORY_LIMIT; i++) s.getState().undo();
    expect(s.getState().design.seed).toBe(51);
  });

  it("drops selected ids that no longer exist", () => {
    const s = createDesignStore();
    s.getState().apply((d) => addNode(d, meta("client"), { x: 0, y: 0 }).design);
    s.getState().select({ nodes: ["client-1"] });
    s.getState().undo();
    expect(s.getState().selection.nodes).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("replace loads a design and clears history", () => {
    const s = createDesignStore();
    s.getState().apply((d) => ({ ...d, seed: 9 }));
    s.getState().replace(emptyDesign("x"));
    expect(s.getState()).toMatchObject({ past: [], future: [], design: { name: "x" } });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement** `apps/web/src/lab/store/design-store.ts`:

```ts
import { createStore } from "zustand/vanilla";
import { emptyDesign, type Design } from "../model/design";
import type { Clipboard } from "../model/ops";

/** R16: at least 100 steps (spec); we keep 200. */
export const HISTORY_LIMIT = 200;

export interface Selection {
  nodes: string[];
  edges: string[];
  groups: string[];
}
const NONE: Selection = { nodes: [], edges: [], groups: [] };

export interface DesignState {
  design: Design;
  past: Design[];
  future: Design[];
  selection: Selection;
  clipboard: Clipboard | null;
  apply(fn: (d: Design) => Design): void;
  replace(d: Design): void;
  undo(): void;
  redo(): void;
  select(sel: Partial<Selection>): void;
  clearSelection(): void;
  setClipboard(clip: Clipboard | null): void;
}

function prune(sel: Selection, d: Design): Selection {
  const nodes = new Set(d.nodes.map((n) => n.id));
  const edges = new Set(d.edges.map((e) => e.id));
  const groups = new Set(d.groups.map((g) => g.id));
  const next = {
    nodes: sel.nodes.filter((id) => nodes.has(id)),
    edges: sel.edges.filter((id) => edges.has(id)),
    groups: sel.groups.filter((id) => groups.has(id)),
  };
  return next.nodes.length === sel.nodes.length && next.edges.length === sel.edges.length && next.groups.length === sel.groups.length
    ? sel
    : next;
}

export function createDesignStore(initial: Design = emptyDesign()) {
  return createStore<DesignState>()((set, get) => ({
    design: initial,
    past: [],
    future: [],
    selection: NONE,
    clipboard: null,
    apply(fn) {
      const { design, past, selection } = get();
      const next = fn(design);
      if (next === design) return;
      set({ design: next, past: [...past, design].slice(-HISTORY_LIMIT), future: [], selection: prune(selection, next) });
    },
    replace(d) {
      set({ design: d, past: [], future: [], selection: NONE });
    },
    undo() {
      const { design, past, future, selection } = get();
      const prev = past.at(-1);
      if (!prev) return;
      set({ design: prev, past: past.slice(0, -1), future: [design, ...future], selection: prune(selection, prev) });
    },
    redo() {
      const { design, past, future, selection } = get();
      const next = future[0];
      if (!next) return;
      set({ design: next, past: [...past, design], future: future.slice(1), selection: prune(selection, next) });
    },
    select(sel) {
      set({ selection: { ...get().selection, ...sel } });
    },
    clearSelection() {
      set({ selection: NONE });
    },
    setClipboard(clip) {
      set({ clipboard: clip });
    },
  }));
}

export type DesignStore = ReturnType<typeof createDesignStore>;
```

- Create: `apps/web/src/lab/store/ui-store.ts`, `apps/web/src/lab/store/LabStores.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lab/canvas/rf-adapter.ts`, `apps/web/src/lab/canvas/rf-adapter.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/lab/canvas/Canvas.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lab/canvas/LabNode.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lab/canvas/LabEdge.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lab/canvas/EdgeLegend.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lab/canvas/icons.ts`, `apps/web/src/lab/labels.ts`, `apps/web/src/lab/labels.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/lab/use-metas.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/lab.spec.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/lab/LabApp.tsx`, `apps/web/messages/{vi,en}.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `createUiStore()` with `{ snap: boolean, minimap: boolean, panelHeight: number, bottomTab: "metrics"|"events"|"logs"|"simulation", connectOpen: boolean, inspectorFocusTick: number }` and setters (`ui-store.ts`).
  - `LabStoresProvider` and hooks `useDesign(selector)`, `useDesignApi()`, `useUi(selector)`, `useUiApi()`. Later tasks add `useCheck`, `useRuns`, `usePlayback` to the same provider.
  - `useMetas() -> ReadonlyMap<string, ComponentMeta>` (empty until the engine is ready).
  - `displayLabel(node, meta, t) -> string` (`labels.ts`).
  - `toFlowNodes(design, ctx)`, `toFlowEdges(design, ctx)`, `toAbsolute(id, relPos, design)` (`rf-adapter.ts`).
  - `<Canvas />` with `data-testid="lab-canvas"`. Nodes carry `data-testid="node-<id>"`, handles `handle-in-<id>` and `handle-out-<id>`, edges `edge-<id>`.

```ts
import { createTranslator } from "next-intl";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import en from "../../messages/en.json";
import vi from "../../messages/vi.json";
import { displayLabel } from "./labels";
import { meta } from "./model/test-metas";

const tEn = createTranslator({ locale: "en", messages: en });
const tVi = createTranslator({ locale: "vi", messages: vi });
const base = { kind: "web-server", replicas: 1, config: {}, position: { x: 0, y: 0 }, groupId: null };

describe("displayLabel", () => {
  it("uses the user's label when set", () => {
    expect(displayLabel({ ...base, id: "web-server-2", label: "API" }, meta("web-server"), tEn)).toBe("API");
  });
  it("falls back to the translated name and number, in the current language", () => {
    const n = { ...base, id: "web-server-2", label: "" };
    expect(displayLabel(n, meta("web-server"), tEn)).toBe("Web server 2");
    expect(displayLabel(n, meta("web-server"), tVi)).toBe("Máy chủ web 2");
  });
  it("shows the id for unknown kinds and odd ids", () => {
    expect(displayLabel({ ...base, kind: "quantum", id: "q", label: "" }, null, tEn)).toBe("q");
    expect(displayLabel({ ...base, id: "custom", label: "" }, meta("web-server"), tEn)).toBe("Web server (custom)");
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/lab/canvas/rf-adapter.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { emptyDesign } from "../model/design";
import { addNode, groupNodes } from "../model/ops";
import { META_BY_KIND, meta } from "../model/test-metas";
import { toAbsolute, toFlowEdges, toFlowNodes } from "./rf-adapter";

function design() {
  let d = emptyDesign();
  d = addNode(d, meta("client"), { x: 0, y: 0 }).design;
  d = addNode(d, meta("lb"), { x: 240, y: 0 }).design;
  return groupNodes(d, ["lb-1"])!.design;
}
const ctx = {
  metas: META_BY_KIND,
  selection: { nodes: ["client-1"], edges: [], groups: [] },
  costs: {},
  issues: { nodes: {}, edges: {} },
  drag: {},
};

describe("React Flow adapter", () => {
  it("puts groups first and gives members a position relative to their frame", () => {
    const d = design();
    const nodes = toFlowNodes(d, ctx);
    expect(nodes.map((n) => [n.id, n.type, n.parentId])).toEqual([
      ["g1", "group", undefined],
      ["client-1", "lab", undefined],
      ["lb-1", "lab", "g1"],
    ]);
    const g = d.groups[0]!;
    expect(nodes[2]!.position).toEqual({ x: 240 - g.position.x, y: 0 - g.position.y });
    expect(nodes[1]!.selected).toBe(true);
  });

  it("converts a member's relative position back to absolute", () => {
    const d = design();
    const g = d.groups[0]!;
    expect(toAbsolute("lb-1", { x: 10, y: 20 }, d)).toEqual({ x: g.position.x + 10, y: g.position.y + 20 });
    expect(toAbsolute("client-1", { x: 10, y: 20 }, d)).toEqual({ x: 10, y: 20 });
  });

  it("marks locked nodes as not deletable and carries the edge kind", () => {
    const d = { ...design(), locked: ["client-1"], edges: [{ id: "e1", from: "client-1", to: "lb-1", kind: "http" as const }] };
    const n = toFlowNodes(d, ctx).find((x) => x.id === "client-1")!;
    expect(n.deletable).toBe(false);
    expect(toFlowEdges(d, ctx)[0]).toMatchObject({ id: "e1", source: "client-1", target: "lb-1", type: "lab", data: { kind: "http" } });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Labels and the adapter.**

`apps/web/src/lab/labels.ts`:

```ts
import type { ComponentMeta } from "@sd/sim";
import type { LabNode } from "./model/design";
import { nodeNumber } from "./model/ids";

type T = (key: string, values?: Record<string, string | number>) => string;

/** R12: the user's label, else the translated component name plus the node's number. */
export function displayLabel(node: Pick<LabNode, "id" | "kind" | "label">, meta: ComponentMeta | null, t: T): string {
  if (node.label.trim() !== "") return node.label;
  if (!meta) return node.id;
  const name = t(meta.labelKey);
  const n = nodeNumber(node.id, node.kind);
  return n === null ? `${name} (${node.id})` : `${name} ${n}`;
}
```

(`t` here is the root translator: `useTranslations()` with no namespace, so `labelKey` values such as `component.web-server` resolve.)

`apps/web/src/lab/canvas/rf-adapter.ts`:

```ts
import type { ComponentMeta, EdgeKind } from "@sd/sim";
import type { Edge as FlowEdge, Node as FlowNode } from "@xyflow/react";
import type { Design, LabGroup, LabNode, XY } from "../model/design";
import type { Selection } from "../store/design-store";

export type NodeState = "normal" | "busy" | "overloaded" | "down";

export interface LabNodeData extends Record<string, unknown> {
  node: LabNode;
  meta: ComponentMeta | null;
  locked: boolean;
  cost: number | null | undefined;
  issues: { blocking: number; warnings: number };
  state?: NodeState;
  /** Trace highlight (Task 28): true = on the path, false = dimmed, undefined = no trace. */
  onPath?: boolean;
  disallowed?: boolean;
}
export interface LabEdgeData extends Record<string, unknown> {
  kind: EdgeKind;
  issues: number;
  onPath?: boolean;
}
export interface GroupData extends Record<string, unknown> {
  group: LabGroup;
}

export interface AdapterContext {
  metas: ReadonlyMap<string, ComponentMeta>;
  selection: Selection;
  costs: Readonly<Record<string, number | null>>;
  issues: { nodes: Readonly<Record<string, { blocking: number; warnings: number }>>; edges: Readonly<Record<string, number>> };
  /** Absolute positions of nodes being dragged (not yet committed). */
  drag: Readonly<Record<string, XY>>;
  states?: Readonly<Record<string, NodeState>>;
  path?: { nodes: ReadonlySet<string>; edges: ReadonlySet<string> } | null;
  allowedKinds?: readonly string[] | null;
}

const NO_ISSUES = { blocking: 0, warnings: 0 };

export function toFlowNodes(d: Design, ctx: AdapterContext): FlowNode[] {
  const groupPos = new Map(d.groups.map((g) => [g.id, g.position]));
  const sel = new Set(ctx.selection.nodes);
  const selGroups = new Set(ctx.selection.groups);
  const groups: FlowNode<GroupData>[] = d.groups.map((g) => ({
    id: g.id,
    type: "group",
    position: g.position,
    data: { group: g },
    style: { width: g.size.width, height: g.size.height },
    selected: selGroups.has(g.id),
    zIndex: -1,
  }));
  const nodes: FlowNode<LabNodeData>[] = d.nodes.map((n) => {
    const abs = ctx.drag[n.id] ?? n.position;
    const parent = n.groupId ? groupPos.get(n.groupId) : undefined;
    const locked = d.locked.includes(n.id);
    return {
      id: n.id,
      type: "lab",
      position: parent ? { x: abs.x - parent.x, y: abs.y - parent.y } : abs,
      parentId: parent ? (n.groupId as string) : undefined,
      selected: sel.has(n.id),
      deletable: !locked,
      data: {
        node: n,
        meta: ctx.metas.get(n.kind) ?? null,
        locked,
        cost: ctx.costs[n.id],
        issues: ctx.issues.nodes[n.id] ?? NO_ISSUES,
        state: ctx.states?.[n.id],
        onPath: ctx.path ? ctx.path.nodes.has(n.id) : undefined,
        disallowed: ctx.allowedKinds ? !ctx.allowedKinds.includes(n.kind) : false,
      },
    };
  });
  return [...groups, ...nodes];
}

export function toFlowEdges(d: Design, ctx: AdapterContext): FlowEdge<LabEdgeData>[] {
  const sel = new Set(ctx.selection.edges);
  return d.edges.map((e) => ({
    id: e.id,
    source: e.from,
    target: e.to,
    sourceHandle: "out",
    targetHandle: "in",
    type: "lab",
    selected: sel.has(e.id),
    data: { kind: e.kind, issues: ctx.issues.edges[e.id] ?? 0, onPath: ctx.path ? ctx.path.edges.has(e.id) : undefined },
  }));
}

/** A React Flow position (relative for group members) as an absolute canvas position. */
export function toAbsolute(id: string, pos: XY, d: Design): XY {
  const n = d.nodes.find((x) => x.id === id);
  const g = n?.groupId ? d.groups.find((x) => x.id === n.groupId) : undefined;
  return g ? { x: g.position.x + pos.x, y: g.position.y + pos.y } : { x: pos.x, y: pos.y };
}
```

`apps/web/src/lab/store/ui-store.ts`:

```ts
import { createStore } from "zustand/vanilla";

export type BottomTab = "metrics" | "events" | "logs" | "simulation";

export interface UiState {
  snap: boolean;
  minimap: boolean;
  panelHeight: number;
  bottomTab: BottomTab;
  connectOpen: boolean;
  /** Incremented to ask the inspector to focus its first control (Enter on a node). */
  inspectorFocusTick: number;
  set(patch: Partial<Omit<UiState, "set" | "focusInspector">>): void;
  focusInspector(): void;
}

export function createUiStore() {
  return createStore<UiState>()((set, get) => ({
    snap: true,
    minimap: true,
    panelHeight: 260,
    bottomTab: "metrics",
    connectOpen: false,
    inspectorFocusTick: 0,
    set: (patch) => set(patch),
    focusInspector: () => set({ inspectorFocusTick: get().inspectorFocusTick + 1 }),
  }));
}
```

`apps/web/src/lab/store/LabStores.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { createContext, useContext, useState } from "react";
import { useStore } from "zustand";
import type { Design } from "../model/design";
import { createDesignStore, type DesignState, type DesignStore } from "./design-store";
import { createUiStore, type UiState } from "./ui-store";

interface Stores {
  design: DesignStore;
  ui: ReturnType<typeof createUiStore>;
}
const Ctx = createContext<Stores | null>(null);

export function LabStoresProvider({ initial, children }: { initial?: Design; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [stores] = useState<Stores>(() => ({ design: createDesignStore(initial), ui: createUiStore() }));
  return <Ctx.Provider value={stores}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
}

function useStores(): Stores {
  const s = useContext(Ctx);
  if (!s) throw new Error("Lab stores used outside LabStoresProvider");
  return s;
}

export const useDesign = <T,>(sel: (s: DesignState) => T): T => useStore(useStores().design, sel);
export const useDesignApi = (): DesignStore => useStores().design;
export const useUi = <T,>(sel: (s: UiState) => T): T => useStore(useStores().ui, sel);
export const useUiApi = () => useStores().ui;
```

`apps/web/src/lab/use-metas.ts`:

```ts
"use client";
import type { ComponentMeta } from "@sd/sim";
import { useMemo } from "react";
import { useEngineStore } from "@/engine/EngineProvider";

const EMPTY: ReadonlyMap<string, ComponentMeta> = new Map();

export function useMetas(): ReadonlyMap<string, ComponentMeta> {
  const components = useEngineStore((s) => s.info?.components);
  return useMemo(() => (components ? new Map(components.map((m) => [m.kind, m])) : EMPTY), [components]);
}
```

```ts
import { Box, Database, MonitorSmartphone, Server, Split, type LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";

const ICONS: Record<string, LucideIcon> = {
  client: MonitorSmartphone,
  lb: Split,
  "web-server": Server,
  postgres: Database,
};
export const iconFor = (kind: string): LucideIcon => ICONS[kind] ?? Box;

/** CSS colour variable per component category (Visual design: icon tiles). */
export const categoryColor = (category: string | undefined): string =>
  category === "compute" || category === "data" || category === "general" ? `var(--sd-cat-${category})` : "var(--sd-cat-general)";
```

`apps/web/src/lab/canvas/LabNode.tsx` (Visual design: "Node card"):

```tsx
"use client";
import { Handle, Position, type NodeProps, type Node } from "@xyflow/react";
import { Gauge, Lock, PowerOff, TriangleAlert } from "lucide-react";
import { useTranslations } from "next-intl";
import { memo } from "react";
import { cn } from "@/ui/cn";
import { displayLabel } from "../labels";
import type { LabNodeData } from "./rf-adapter";
import { categoryColor, iconFor } from "./icons";

const STATE_CLASS = {
  normal: "",
  busy: "border-2 border-busy bg-busy-soft",
  overloaded: "border-2 border-over bg-over-soft motion-safe:animate-[sd-pulse_1.2s_ease-in-out_infinite]",
  down: "border-2 border-down bg-down-soft [&>*]:opacity-55",
} as const;
const STATE_ICON = { busy: Gauge, overloaded: TriangleAlert, down: PowerOff } as const;

function LabNodeView({ data, selected }: NodeProps<Node<LabNodeData>>) {
  const t = useTranslations();
  const { node, meta, locked, cost, issues, state = "normal", onPath, disallowed } = data;
  const Icon = iconFor(node.kind);
  const StateIcon = state !== "normal" ? STATE_ICON[state] : null;
  const label = displayLabel(node, meta, t);
  const color = categoryColor(meta?.category);
  return (
    <div
      data-testid={`node-${node.id}`}
      data-state={state}
      aria-label={label}
      className={cn(
        "relative h-[76px] w-[184px] rounded-lg border bg-surface shadow-1",
        locked ? "border-dashed border-border-strong" : "border-border",
        STATE_CLASS[state],
        selected && "outline-2 outline-offset-2 outline-accent",
        onPath === true && "shadow-[0_0_0_6px_var(--sd-accent-soft)]",
        onPath === false && "opacity-35",
        disallowed && "opacity-50",
      )}
    >
      <Handle id="in" type="target" position={Position.Left} data-testid={`handle-in-${node.id}`} className="!size-2.5 !border-[1.5px] !border-border-strong !bg-surface" />
      <div className="absolute top-2 -left-3 flex flex-col gap-0.5" aria-hidden>
        {meta?.accepts.map((k) => (
          <span key={k} className="size-1.5 rounded-full" style={{ background: `var(--sd-edge-${k})` }} />
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-2.5 pt-2">
        <span className="flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-sm" style={{ color, background: `color-mix(in srgb, ${color} 14%, transparent)` }}>
          <Icon size={16} aria-hidden />
        </span>
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="truncate text-sm font-semibold">{label}</div>
          <div className="truncate text-2xs text-text-3">{meta ? t(meta.labelKey) : node.kind}</div>
        </div>
        {locked ? <Lock size={14} className="text-text-3" aria-label={t("lab.node.locked")} /> : null}
        {StateIcon ? <StateIcon size={14} aria-hidden className={state === "busy" ? "text-busy" : state === "overloaded" ? "text-over" : "text-down"} /> : null}
      </div>
      <div className="mx-2.5 mt-1.5 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-border pt-1">
        <span className="num rounded-xs bg-surface-2 px-1 text-2xs">{t("lab.node.replicas", { count: node.replicas })}</span>
        <span className="num text-2xs text-text-2">
          {typeof cost === "number" ? t("lab.node.costPerMonth", { cost: Math.round(cost) }) : t("lab.node.costUnknown")}
        </span>
      </div>
      {issues.blocking + issues.warnings > 0 ? (
        <span
          className={cn(
            "num absolute -top-2 -right-2 flex size-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[10px] font-semibold text-on-accent",
            issues.blocking > 0 ? "bg-over" : "bg-busy",
          )}
          aria-label={t("lab.node.issues", { count: issues.blocking + issues.warnings })}
        >
          {issues.blocking + issues.warnings}
        </span>
      ) : null}
      <Handle id="out" type="source" position={Position.Right} data-testid={`handle-out-${node.id}`} className="!size-2.5 !border-[1.5px] !border-border-strong !bg-surface" />
    </div>
  );
}

export const LabNode = memo(LabNodeView);
```

`apps/web/src/lab/canvas/LabEdge.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { BaseEdge, getSmoothStepPath, type Edge, type EdgeProps } from "@xyflow/react";
import { memo } from "react";
import type { LabEdgeData } from "./rf-adapter";

function LabEdgeView({ id, sourceX, sourceY, targetX, targetY, sourcePosition, targetPosition, data, selected }: EdgeProps<Edge<LabEdgeData>>) {
  const kind = data?.kind ?? "http";
  const [path] = getSmoothStepPath({ sourceX, sourceY, sourcePosition, targetX, targetY, targetPosition, borderRadius: 8 });
  const color = `var(--sd-edge-${kind})`;
  return (
    <g data-testid={`edge-${id}`} data-kind={kind} opacity={data?.onPath === false ? 0.3 : 1}>
      {selected || data?.onPath ? <path d={path} fill="none" stroke="var(--sd-accent-soft)" strokeWidth={8} /> : null}
      <BaseEdge
        id={id}
        path={path}
        markerEnd={`url(#sd-arrow-${kind})`}
        style={{ stroke: color, strokeWidth: selected ? 3 : 2, strokeDasharray: `var(--sd-dash-${kind})`, strokeLinecap: "round" }}
      />
    </g>
  );
}

export const LabEdge = memo(LabEdgeView);

/** One arrowhead per kind; rendered once inside the canvas. */
export function EdgeMarkers() {
  return (
    <svg width="0" height="0" aria-hidden className="absolute">
      <defs>
        {(["http", "db", "cache", "msg"] as const).map((k) => (
          <marker key={k} id={`sd-arrow-${k}`} viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="9" refY="5" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="8" orient="auto-start-reverse">
            <path d="M0 0 L10 5 L0 10 z" fill={`var(--sd-edge-${k})`} />
          </marker>
        ))}
      </defs>
    </svg>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/lab/canvas/EdgeLegend.tsx`: a `surface` card (radius lg, border, `shadow-1`, padding 8) at the top-left of the canvas, titled `t("lab.legend.title")` as a section label. One row per `EDGE_KINDS` entry: a 24 × 8 SVG with a line in `var(--sd-edge-<k>)` and `strokeDasharray: var(--sd-dash-<k>)`, then `t("edgeKind.<k>")`. Rows carry `data-testid="legend-<k>"`.

`apps/web/src/lab/canvas/Canvas.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import {
  Background,
  BackgroundVariant,
  MiniMap,
  ReactFlow,
  type Node as FlowNode,
  type NodeChange,
  type EdgeChange,
} from "@xyflow/react";
import "@xyflow/react/dist/base.css";
import { useTranslations } from "next-intl";
import { useCallback, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import type { XY } from "../model/design";
import { moveGroup, moveNodes } from "../model/ops";
import { useDesign, useDesignApi, useUi } from "../store/LabStores";
import { useMetas } from "../use-metas";
import { EdgeLegend } from "./EdgeLegend";
import { EdgeMarkers, LabEdge } from "./LabEdge";
import { LabNode } from "./LabNode";
import { toAbsolute, toFlowEdges, toFlowNodes, type AdapterContext } from "./rf-adapter";

const nodeTypes = { lab: LabNode };
const edgeTypes = { lab: LabEdge };

export function Canvas({ extra }: { extra?: Partial<AdapterContext> }) {
  const t = useTranslations("lab");
  const design = useDesign((s) => s.design);
  const selection = useDesign((s) => s.selection);
  const api = useDesignApi();
  const metas = useMetas();
  const snap = useUi((s) => s.snap);
  const minimap = useUi((s) => s.minimap);
  const [drag, setDrag] = useState<Record<string, XY>>({});

  const ctx: AdapterContext = {
    metas,
    selection,
    costs: {},
    issues: { nodes: {}, edges: {} },
    drag,
    ...extra,
  };
  const nodes = useMemo(() => toFlowNodes(design, ctx), [design, ctx.selection, ctx.costs, ctx.issues, ctx.drag, ctx.states, ctx.path, ctx.metas]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  const edges = useMemo(() => toFlowEdges(design, ctx), [design, ctx.selection, ctx.issues, ctx.path]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  const onNodesChange = useCallback(
    (changes: NodeChange[]) => {
      const d = api.getState().design;
      const dragging: Record<string, XY> = {};
      const commit: Record<string, XY> = {};
      const groupMoves: [string, XY][] = [];
      let selNodes: string[] | null = null;
      let selGroups: string[] | null = null;
      for (const c of changes) {
        if (c.type === "position" && c.position) {
          if (d.groups.some((g) => g.id === c.id)) {
            if (!c.dragging) groupMoves.push([c.id, c.position]);
            continue;
          }
          const abs = toAbsolute(c.id, c.position, d);
          if (c.dragging) dragging[c.id] = abs;
          else commit[c.id] = abs; // keyboard arrows and drag end: one history step
        } else if (c.type === "select") {
          const isGroup = d.groups.some((g) => g.id === c.id);
          const base = isGroup ? (selGroups ??= [...api.getState().selection.groups]) : (selNodes ??= [...api.getState().selection.nodes]);
          const i = base.indexOf(c.id);
          if (c.selected && i < 0) base.push(c.id);
          if (!c.selected && i >= 0) base.splice(i, 1);
        }
      }
      if (Object.keys(dragging).length) setDrag((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...dragging }));
      if (Object.keys(commit).length || groupMoves.length) {
        api.getState().apply((x) => groupMoves.reduce((acc, [id, p]) => moveGroup(acc, id, p), moveNodes(x, commit)));
        setDrag({});
      }
      if (selNodes || selGroups) {
        api.getState().select({ ...(selNodes ? { nodes: selNodes } : {}), ...(selGroups ? { groups: selGroups } : {}) });
      }
    },
    [api],
  );

  const onNodeDragStop = useCallback(
    (_e: unknown, _n: FlowNode, dragged: FlowNode[]) => {
      const d = api.getState().design;
      const commit: Record<string, XY> = {};
      for (const n of dragged) if (n.type === "lab") commit[n.id] = toAbsolute(n.id, n.position, d);
      api.getState().apply((x) => moveNodes(x, commit));
      setDrag({});
    },
    [api],
  );

  const onEdgesChange = useCallback(
    (changes: EdgeChange[]) => {
      let edgesSel: string[] | null = null;
      for (const c of changes) {
        if (c.type !== "select") continue;
        edgesSel ??= [...api.getState().selection.edges];
        const i = edgesSel.indexOf(c.id);
        if (c.selected && i < 0) edgesSel.push(c.id);
        if (!c.selected && i >= 0) edgesSel.splice(i, 1);
      }
      if (edgesSel) api.getState().select({ edges: edgesSel });
    },
    [api],
  );

  return (
    <div data-testid="lab-canvas" aria-label={t("canvas")} className="relative h-full w-full bg-canvas">
      <EdgeMarkers />
      <ReactFlow
        nodes={nodes}
        edges={edges}
        nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
        edgeTypes={edgeTypes}
        onNodesChange={onNodesChange}
        onEdgesChange={onEdgesChange}
        onNodeDragStop={onNodeDragStop}
        snapToGrid={snap}
        snapGrid={[16, 16]}
        deleteKeyCode={null}
        selectionKeyCode="Shift"
        multiSelectionKeyCode={["Meta", "Control", "Shift"]}
        fitView
        minZoom={0.25}
        maxZoom={2}
        proOptions={{ hideAttribution: true }}
      >
        <Background variant={BackgroundVariant.Dots} gap={16} size={1} color="var(--sd-grid)" />
        {minimap ? (
          <MiniMap
            ariaLabel={t("minimap")}
            pannable
            zoomable
            className="!m-3 !h-28 !w-42 overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-border !bg-surface/85"
            nodeColor={(n) => (n.type === "group" ? "transparent" : "var(--sd-cat-compute)")}
          />
        ) : null}
      </ReactFlow>
      <div className="pointer-events-none absolute top-3 left-3">
        <div className="pointer-events-auto">
          <EdgeLegend />
        </div>
      </div>
      {design.nodes.length === 0 ? <EmptyState /> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

function EmptyState() {
  const t = useTranslations("lab.empty");
  return (
    <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 text-center">
      <p className="text-md font-semibold">{t("title")}</p>
      <p className="text-sm text-text-2">{t("body", { shortcut: "⌘K" })}</p>
    </div>
  );
}
```

(`hideAttribution` is allowed by React Flow's MIT license; credit for React Flow goes in the About dialog's text, added in Task 33. The ⌘K shown here becomes platform-aware in Task 16.)
