- [ ] **Step 2: Geometry** `apps/web/src/lab/canvas/edge-geometry.ts`:

```ts
export interface Track {
  /** x,y pairs */
  pts: Float32Array;
  /** cumulative length at each point */
  cum: Float32Array;
  length: number;
}

const CURVE_STEPS = 8;

/** Samples an SVG path made of M, L and Q commands (what getSmoothStepPath emits). */
export function samplePath(d: string): Track {
  const tokens = d.match(/[MLQ]|-?\d*\.?\d+(?:e[-+]?\d+)?/gi) ?? [];
  const xs: number[] = [];
  const ys: number[] = [];
  let i = 0;
  let cmd = "M";
  const num = () => Number(tokens[i++]);
  while (i < tokens.length) {
    if (/^[MLQ]$/i.test(tokens[i]!)) cmd = tokens[i++]!.toUpperCase();
    if (cmd === "M" || cmd === "L") {
      xs.push(num());
      ys.push(num());
    } else if (cmd === "Q") {
      const x0 = xs.at(-1) ?? 0;
      const y0 = ys.at(-1) ?? 0;
      const cx = num(), cy = num(), x1 = num(), y1 = num();
      for (let s = 1; s <= CURVE_STEPS; s++) {
        const t = s / CURVE_STEPS;
        const u = 1 - t;
        xs.push(u * u * x0 + 2 * u * t * cx + t * t * x1);
        ys.push(u * u * y0 + 2 * u * t * cy + t * t * y1);
      }
    }
  }
  const n = xs.length;
  const pts = new Float32Array(n * 2);
  const cum = new Float32Array(n);
  for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
    pts[2 * k] = xs[k]!;
    pts[2 * k + 1] = ys[k]!;
    if (k > 0) cum[k] = cum[k - 1]! + Math.hypot(xs[k]! - xs[k - 1]!, ys[k]! - ys[k - 1]!);
  }
  return { pts, cum, length: n ? cum[n - 1]! : 0 };
}

/** Writes the point at distance `dist` along the track into `out` (no allocation). */
export function pointAt(t: Track, dist: number, out: { x: number; y: number }): void {
  const n = t.cum.length;
  if (n === 0) return;
  const d = Math.max(0, Math.min(t.length, dist));
  let lo = 0;
  let hi = n - 1;
  while (lo < hi) {
    const mid = (lo + hi) >> 1;
    if (t.cum[mid]! < d) lo = mid + 1;
    else hi = mid;
  }
  if (lo === 0) {
    out.x = t.pts[0]!;
    out.y = t.pts[1]!;
    return;
  }
  const a = lo - 1;
  const seg = t.cum[lo]! - t.cum[a]!;
  const f = seg > 0 ? (d - t.cum[a]!) / seg : 0;
  out.x = t.pts[2 * a]! + (t.pts[2 * lo]! - t.pts[2 * a]!) * f;
  out.y = t.pts[2 * a + 1]! + (t.pts[2 * lo + 1]! - t.pts[2 * a + 1]!) * f;
}

/** Paths as LabEdge drew them, keyed by edge id. */
const paths = new Map<string, string>();
let version = 0;
export const edgePaths = {
  set(id: string, d: string) {
    if (paths.get(id) !== d) {
      paths.set(id, d);
      version++;
    }
  },
  delete(id: string) {
    if (paths.delete(id)) version++;
  },
  get: (id: string) => paths.get(id),
  version: () => version,
};
```

In `LabEdge.tsx`, register the drawn path:

    edgePaths.set(id, path);
  }, [id, path]);
  useLayoutEffect(() => () => edgePaths.delete(id), [id]);

- [ ] **Step 3: Dots** `apps/web/src/lab/canvas/dots.ts`:

```ts
import type { TimelineView } from "@sd/sim";

/** Dots per simulated second on the busiest edge of the run (R19). */
export const MAX_DOTS_PER_SECOND = 12;
/** Travel speed of a dot, canvas pixels per simulated second. */
export const DOT_SPEED = 240;

/** Visual approximation (R19): rate ∝ target inFlight / target in-degree, scaled to the run's max. */
export function edgeRates(tl: TimelineView, edges: readonly { id: string; to: string }[]): { perFrame: Float32Array; edgeCount: number } {
  const E = edges.length;
  const perFrame = new Float32Array(tl.frames * E);
  const indegree = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const e of edges) indegree.set(e.to, (indegree.get(e.to) ?? 0) + 1);
  const known = new Set(tl.nodeIds);
  let max = 0;
  for (let f = 0; f < tl.frames; f++) {
    for (let k = 0; k < E; k++) {
      const e = edges[k]!;
      const v = known.has(e.to) ? tl.value(f, e.to, "inFlight") / indegree.get(e.to)! : 0;
      perFrame[f * E + k] = v;
      if (v > max) max = v;
    }
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < perFrame.length; i++) {
    const v = perFrame[i]!;
    perFrame[i] = v <= 0 || max <= 0 ? 0 : Math.max(1, (v / max) * MAX_DOTS_PER_SECOND);
  }
  return { perFrame, edgeCount: E };
}

/** Fixed-capacity pool of dots per edge; progress in [0, 1], -1 = free. No allocation after construction. */
export class DotField {
  readonly progress: Float32Array;
  private readonly acc: Float32Array;

  constructor(readonly edgeCount: number, readonly capacity = 24) {
    this.progress = new Float32Array(edgeCount * capacity).fill(-1);
    this.acc = new Float32Array(edgeCount);
  }

  reset(): void {
    this.progress.fill(-1);
    this.acc.fill(0);
  }

  /** Advance by `dtSimMs` of simulated time using rates[offset + edge] and edge pixel lengths. */
  step(dtSimMs: number, rates: Float32Array, offset: number, lengths: Float32Array): void {
    const dt = dtSimMs / 1000;
    const cap = this.capacity;
    for (let e = 0; e < this.edgeCount; e++) {
      const len = lengths[e]!;
      const base = e * cap;
      const dp = len > 0 ? (DOT_SPEED * dt) / len : 1;
      for (let s = 0; s < cap; s++) {
        const p = this.progress[base + s]!;
        if (p < 0) continue;
        const next = p + dp;
        this.progress[base + s] = next > 1 ? -1 : next;
      }
      this.acc[e] = this.acc[e]! + rates[offset + e]! * dt;
      while (this.acc[e]! >= 1) {
        this.acc[e] = this.acc[e]! - 1;
        for (let s = 0; s < cap; s++) {
          if (this.progress[base + s]! < 0) {
            this.progress[base + s] = 0;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  forEach(cb: (edge: number, progress: number) => void): void {
    const cap = this.capacity;
    for (let i = 0; i < this.progress.length; i++) {
      const p = this.progress[i]!;
      if (p >= 0) cb((i / cap) | 0, p);
    }
  }
}
```

(The test's first step spawns 5 dots in 500 ms at 10/s; each spawned dot starts at progress 0 and has not moved yet. Its `forEach` callback counts dots with `p >= 0`.)

- [ ] **Step 4: The layer** `apps/web/src/lab/canvas/DotsLayer.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useStoreApi } from "@xyflow/react";
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import type { EdgeKind } from "@sd/sim";
import { frameAt } from "../run/clock";
import { useDesignApi, usePlaybackApi } from "../store/LabStores";
import { usePlaybackView } from "../run/use-playback-view";
import { DotField, edgeRates } from "./dots";
import { edgePaths, pointAt, samplePath, type Track } from "./edge-geometry";

const KINDS: EdgeKind[] = ["http", "db", "cache", "msg"];

/** Request dots above React Flow on one canvas (spec §5.4). No React work per frame. */
export function DotsLayer() {
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const flow = useStoreApi();
  const playback = usePlaybackApi();
  const design = useDesignApi();
  const view = usePlaybackView();
  const run = view?.active ? view.run : null;

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas || !run) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d")!;
    const edges = design.getState().design.edges.map((e) => ({ id: e.id, to: e.to, kind: e.kind }));
    const { perFrame } = edgeRates(run.timeline, edges);
    const field = new DotField(edges.length, 24);
    const lengths = new Float32Array(edges.length);
    const tracks: (Track | null)[] = edges.map(() => null);
    const kindIndex = edges.map((e) => KINDS.indexOf(e.kind));
    const colors: string[] = [];
    const point = { x: 0, y: 0 };
    let pathsVersion = -1;
    let lastTime = playback.getState().timeMs;
    let raf = 0;
    const reduced = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;

    const readColors = () => {
      const cs = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);
      KINDS.forEach((k, i) => (colors[i] = cs.getPropertyValue(`--sd-edge-${k}`).trim()));
    };
    const resize = () => {
      const r = canvas.parentElement!.getBoundingClientRect();
      const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
      canvas.width = Math.round(r.width * dpr);
      canvas.height = Math.round(r.height * dpr);
      canvas.style.width = `${r.width}px`;
      canvas.style.height = `${r.height}px`;
    };
    const refreshTracks = () => {
      if (edgePaths.version() === pathsVersion) return;
      pathsVersion = edgePaths.version();
      edges.forEach((e, k) => {
        const d = edgePaths.get(e.id);
        tracks[k] = d ? samplePath(d) : null;
        lengths[k] = tracks[k]?.length ?? 0;
      });
    };

    const draw = () => {
      const [tx, ty, zoom] = flow.getState().transform;
      const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
      ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      const radius = Math.max(2, 3 * Math.min(zoom, 1.25));
      for (let kind = 0; kind < KINDS.length; kind++) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        field.forEach((e, p) => {
          if (kindIndex[e] !== kind) return;
          const t = tracks[e];
          if (!t) return;
          pointAt(t, p * t.length, point);
          const x = point.x * zoom + tx;
          const y = point.y * zoom + ty;
          ctx.moveTo(x + radius, y);
          ctx.arc(x, y, radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        });
        ctx.fillStyle = colors[kind]!;
        ctx.fill();
      }
    };

    const frame = () => {
      refreshTracks();
      const s = playback.getState();
      const dt = s.timeMs - lastTime;
      lastTime = s.timeMs;
      if (dt < 0 || dt > 1000) field.reset(); // a seek: start clean
      else if (dt > 0) {
        const f = frameAt(s.timeMs, run.timeline.frameMs, run.timeline.frames);
        field.step(dt, perFrame, f * edges.length, lengths);
      }
      draw();
      raf = s.playing ? requestAnimationFrame(frame) : 0;
    };

    readColors();
    resize();
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(() => (resize(), draw()));
    ro.observe(canvas.parentElement!);
    const mo = new MutationObserver(() => (readColors(), draw()));
    mo.observe(document.documentElement, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["data-theme"] });
    const unsubPlayback = playback.subscribe((s, prev) => {
      if (s.playing && !raf) raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      else if (!s.playing && s.timeMs !== prev.timeMs && !raf) frame();
    });
    const unsubFlow = flow.subscribe((s, prev) => {
      if (s.transform !== prev.transform && !raf) draw();
    });
    frame();
    void reduced; // trails are not drawn at all, so reduced motion needs no special case here

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      ro.disconnect();
      mo.disconnect();
      unsubPlayback();
      unsubFlow();
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    };
  }, [run, flow, playback, design]);

  return <canvas ref={canvasRef} aria-hidden className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 z-[4]" />;
}
```

(Remove the `reduced` lines if lint flags them as unused; dots never draw trails, so the reduced-motion rule of R19 is met by `globals.css` disabling the pulse.)

Mount `<DotsLayer />` inside the canvas wrapper, after `<ReactFlow>` and before the legend. React Flow's pane uses z-index 0–3 for edges and nodes in its default stacking. If dots render under the nodes, raise the z-index and check that nodes stay clickable (the canvas has `pointer-events: none`).

### Task 28: Follow a request (Phase 4 gate)

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/lab/run/trace-model.ts`, `apps/web/src/lab/run/trace-model.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/lab/run/TracePanel.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lab/run/TracePanel.test.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lab/store/trace-store.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `filterTraces(summaries, outcome | null) -> TraceSummary[]` (sorted by latency, slowest first; ties by id); `tracePath(trace, design) -> { nodes: Set<string>; edges: Set<string> }` (an edge is on the path when two consecutive steps go from its `from` to its `to`); `hopList(trace) -> { nodeId, action, tMs, waitMs, serviceMs }[]` (`trace-model.ts`).
  - Trace store `{ runId, traceId, trace, hop, outcome }` with `pick(runId, traceId, trace)`, `setHop(i)`, `clear()`, `setOutcome(o)`.
  - `<TracePanel />`: an outcome filter, the trace list, the step list. `[` / `]` move between hops and seek playback to the hop's time.

```ts
import { sim } from "@sd/sim/node";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { designFromInput, fourTier } from "@/engine/test-fixtures";
import { filterTraces, hopList, tracePath } from "./trace-model";

describe("trace model", () => {
  const input = fourTier(3000);
  const run = sim.simulate(input);
  const summaries = run.traceSummaries();

  it("sorts the slowest first and filters by outcome", () => {
    const all = filterTraces(summaries, null);
    expect(all).toHaveLength(summaries.length);
    for (let i = 1; i < all.length; i++) expect(all[i - 1]!.latencyMs).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(all[i]!.latencyMs);
    expect(filterTraces(summaries, "delivered").every((s) => s.outcome === "delivered")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("maps a delivered request onto the design's nodes and edges", () => {
    const delivered = filterTraces(summaries, "delivered")[0]!;
    const trace = run.trace(summaries.indexOf(delivered));
    const path = tracePath(trace, designFromInput(input));
    expect([...path.nodes]).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(["users", "lb", "web"]));
    expect(path.edges.has("e1")).toBe(true);
    expect(path.edges.has("e2")).toBe(true);
    const hops = hopList(trace);
    expect(hops[0]).toMatchObject({ nodeId: "users", action: "send" });
    expect(hops.every((h) => h.waitMs >= 0 && h.serviceMs >= 0)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/lab/run/TracePanel.test.tsx` (jsdom): given summaries and a fake `loadTrace`, the list shows "#3 · read · 12 ms" items sorted slowest first; choosing "Timed out" filters; clicking an item calls `loadTrace(id)` and then shows the hops; `]` and `[` move the highlighted hop (`aria-current`) and call `onSeek(tMs)`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `apps/web/src/lab/run/trace-model.ts`:

```ts
import type { Trace, TraceOutcome, TraceSummary } from "@sd/sim";
import type { Design } from "../model/design";

export function filterTraces(s: readonly TraceSummary[], outcome: TraceOutcome | null): TraceSummary[] {
  return s
    .filter((x) => outcome === null || x.outcome === outcome)
    .sort((a, b) => b.latencyMs - a.latencyMs || a.id - b.id);
}

export function tracePath(trace: Trace, d: Design): { nodes: Set<string>; edges: Set<string> } {
  const nodes = new Set(trace.steps.map((s) => s.nodeId));
  const edges = new Set<string>();
  for (let i = 1; i < trace.steps.length; i++) {
    const from = trace.steps[i - 1]!.nodeId;
    const to = trace.steps[i]!.nodeId;
    if (from === to) continue;
    const e = d.edges.find((x) => x.from === from && x.to === to);
    if (e) edges.add(e.id);
  }
  return { nodes, edges };
}

export const hopList = (trace: Trace) =>
  trace.steps.map((s) => ({ nodeId: s.nodeId, action: s.action, tMs: s.tMs, waitMs: s.waitMs, serviceMs: s.serviceMs }));
```

`designFromInput(input)` in `src/engine/test-fixtures.ts` turns a `SimInput` into a `Design`: nodes on one row (x = 240 × index), `groupId: null`, and the same edges, workload, faults, duration and seed.

`trace-store.ts`: as in the interface; add it to `LabStores`. `TracePanel.tsx`: an outcome `Segmented` (All plus the four outcomes), a scrollable list (`role="listbox"`) of `filterTraces` items (`trace.item` with the translated class and `formatMetric("p95", latencyMs)`), and on pick `client.request({ type: "trace", runId, index })`. The index is the position in the run's `traces` array, because the worker's `trace(i)` indexes the engine's trace list. Then the hop list as an ordered list: each hop shows `trace.hop` (node label, `t("traceAction.<a>")`), `wait`, `service`, and the active hop has `aria-current="step"` and an accent bar. Prev/next `IconButton`s and the `prevHop`/`nextHop` shortcuts move the hop and seek playback to `tMs`. "Stop following" clears it. When the current run changes, the trace store clears.

`Canvas` passes `path = tracePath(...)` into the adapter context while a trace is picked, so nodes and edges on the path get the halo and the others dim. The active hop's node also gets the selection outline.

In `LabApp`, the right column becomes Radix Tabs: "Inspect" (the inspector) and "Requests" (`TracePanel`, `data-tour="requests"`).

- [ ] **Step 4: E2E (spec §8 scenario 2)** append to `apps/web/e2e/playback.spec.ts`:

```ts
test("run, scrub, follow a request and read the metrics", async ({ page }) => {
  await openLab(page);
  await buildFourTier(page);
  await runOnce(page);

  // Scrub halfway with the keyboard and check the readout.
  const scrub = page.getByRole("slider", { name: "Playback time" });
  await scrub.focus();
  for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) await page.keyboard.press("ArrowRight");
  await expect(scrub).toHaveAttribute("aria-valuetext", "5.0 / 10.0 s");

  // Play briefly, then pause.
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Play" }).click();
  await page.waitForTimeout(400);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Pause" }).click();
  expect(Number(await scrub.getAttribute("aria-valuenow"))).toBeGreaterThan(5000);

  // Follow the slowest delivered request.
  await page.getByRole("tab", { name: "Requests" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: "Answered" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("listbox", { name: "Sampled requests" }).getByRole("option").first().click();
  const hops = page.getByRole("list", { name: "Hops" }).getByRole("listitem");
  await expect(hops.first()).toContainText("Users 1");
  await expect(page.getByTestId("node-lb-1")).not.toHaveClass(/opacity-35/);
  await page.keyboard.press("]");
  await expect(hops.nth(1)).toHaveAttribute("aria-current", "step");

  // Metrics are there.
  await page.getByRole("tab", { name: "Metrics" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("metric-availability")).toContainText("%");
});
```
